end

genv.OutlawResolveUIHost = function()
    local cloneref = genv.cloneref
    local PlayerGui = lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") or (type(lp.WaitForChild) == "function" and lp:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 5))
    if type(cloneref) == "function" and PlayerGui then
        local ok, res = pcall(cloneref, PlayerGui)
        if ok and res then PlayerGui = res end
    end
    if PlayerGui then genv.OutlawUIHost = PlayerGui return PlayerGui end
    local cached = genv.OutlawUIHost
    if typeof(cached) == "Instance" and cached.Parent ~= nil and cached.Name ~= "RobloxGui" then return cached end
    local candidates, seen = {}, {}
    local function push(inst)
        if typeof(inst) == "Instance" and inst.Name ~= "RobloxGui" and not seen[inst] then
            seen[inst] = true table.insert(candidates, inst)
        end
    end
    for _, name in ipairs({ "gethui", "get_hidden_gui", "gethiddengui" }) do
        local fn = genv[name]
        if type(fn) == "function" then
            local ok, res = pcall(fn)
            if ok then push(res) end
        end
    end
    local ok, core = pcall(function()
        local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
        return type(cloneref) == "function" and cloneref(CoreGui) or CoreGui
    end)
    if ok then push(core) end
    for _, inst in ipairs(candidates) do
        local probeOk = pcall(function()
            local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
            sg.Name = "OutlawProbe" sg.ResetOnSpawn = false sg.Parent = inst
            local f = Instance.new("Frame") f.Parent = sg f:Destroy() sg:Destroy()
        end)
        if probeOk then genv.OutlawUIHost = inst return inst end
    end
    genv.OutlawUIHost = nil
    return nil, "UI host unavailable"
end

genv.OutlawParentGui = function(gui)
    local host = genv.OutlawResolveUIHost()
    if not host or typeof(gui) ~= "Instance" then return false end
    if gui:IsA("ScreenGui") then pcall(function() gui.ResetOnSpawn = false end) end
    genv["OutlawStored_" .. tostring(gui.Name)] = gui
    local syn = genv.syn
    local protect = syn and syn.protect_gui or genv.protect_gui
    if type(protect) == "function" then pcall(protect, gui) end
    local ok, err = pcall(function() gui.Parent = host end)
    return ok, err
end

local function v229(p39)
    local v230 = tostring(p39 or ""):lower():gsub("[%s,$]", ""):gsub("/s$", "")
    if v230 == "" then return 0, "0" end
    local t9 = { k = 1000, m = 1000000, b = 1e9, t = 1e12, q = 1e15 }
    local match, v232 = v230:match("^([%d]+%.?[%d]*)([kmbtq]?)$")
    local v233 = tonumber(match)
    if v233 then return math.max(0, math.floor(v233 * (t9[v232] or 1) + 0.5)), v230 end
    return nil
end

    t41._installClaimPresentationFilter = function(p182)
        if p182.claimPresentationNode then return true end

        for _, v in ipairs(EggState2.FieldClaimed:GetConnections()) do
            local orig = type(v) == "table" and rawget(v, "_fn") or nil
            if v679(orig):find("PlayerScripts.Game.AreaEggs", 1, true) and v671(orig, { "Bottom","Message","Color","Time" }) then
                p182.claimPresentationNode = v p182.claimPresentationOriginal = orig
                local function wrapper(p183)
                    local supp = p182.claimPresentationSuppression
                    if supp and supp.expiresAt < os.clock() then p182.claimPresentationSuppression = nil supp = nil end
                    if not p182.destroyed and p182.enabled and supp and type(p183) == "table" and p183.AssetCategory == supp.assetCategory then p182.claimPresentationSuppression = nil return end
                    return orig(p183)
                end
                p182.claimPresentationWrapper = wrapper v._fn = wrapper
                return true
            end
        end
        return false
    end

    end

        local v803

            local minV = config.minValuePerSec or 0
            if minV > 0 then
                v803 = v670(p198)
                if not v803 or v803 < minV then return false end
            end
            return true
        end
        if #config.names == 0 or not v665(config.names, v801.name) and not v665(config.names, v801.category) then return false end
        if config.areaEnabled and not (#config.areas ~= 0 and v665(config.areas, p198.AreaId)) then return false end
        if (config.minValuePerSec or 0) > 0 then
            v803 = v670(p198)
            if not v803 or v803 < config.minValuePerSec then return false end
        end
        return true
    end
    t41._matches = function(p199, p200)
        if p200.State ~= AreaEggs.States.Slot and p200.State ~= AreaEggs.States.Dropped then return false end
        if p199.riftObjectiveEnabled and p199:_isRiftObjective(p200) then return true end
        if p199.userStealEnabled and p199:_matchesNormalFilter(p200) then return true end
        return false
    end
    t41._isForeignCarryCandidate = function(p201, p202)
        local sFP = p201.isPremium and p201.config.stealFromPlayers
        return sFP and p201.userStealEnabled and type(p202) == "table" and p202.State == AreaEggs.States.Carried and type(p202.CarrierUserId) == "number" and p202.CarrierUserId ~= LocalPlayer2.UserId and not p201:isIntentionalGiftDrop(p202.Uid) and p201:_matchesNormalFilter(p202)
    end
    t41._resolveForeignCarrier = function(p203, p204, p205)
        if not p203:_isForeignCarryCandidate(p204) then return nil end
        local playerByUserId = Players2:GetPlayerByUserId(p204.CarrierUserId)
        local c = playerByUserId and playerByUserId.Character
        local hrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not playerByUserId or not hrp or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return nil end
        if playerByUserId:GetAttribute("InBossArena") == true then return nil end
        local ragdollEnd = tonumber(playerByUserId:GetAttribute("RagdollEndTime")) or 0
        if workspace:GetServerTimeNow() < ragdollEnd then return nil end
        if p205 or ToolGameplayGuard.IsInsideArena(playerByUserId) and not ToolGameplayGuard.IsInsideSafeZone(playerByUserId) then return playerByUserId, hrp, hum end
        return nil
    end
    t41.isPrimerEgg = function(p206, p207)

        return false
    end
    t41.hasMatchingUserEggs = function(p208)
        if p208.enabled then
            if p208.userStealEnabled and not p208.destroyed then
                if not p208.drScrambleRouteActive and (p208.running or p208.activeTarget ~= nil or p208.tweenRecoveryUid ~= nil) then return true end
                for _, record in pairs(p208.records) do
                    if record.State ~= AreaEggs.States.Slot and record.State ~= AreaEggs.States.Dropped then
                        if p208:_isForeignCarryCandidate(record) then return true end
                        continue
                    end
                    if p208:_matchesNormalFilter(record) then return true end
                end
                return false
            end
        end
        return false
    end

    t41.hasDrPriorityEggsAuthoritative = function(p210, p211)

        local ok, result = pcall(EggState2.ReadFieldEggs)
        local Records = ok and type(result) == "table" and (type(result.Records) == "table" and result.Records or result) or p210.records
        for _, Record in pairs(Records) do

            if Record.State ~= AreaEggs.States.Slot and Record.State ~= AreaEggs.States.Dropped then continue end
            if p210:_matchesNormalFilter(Record) then return true end
        end
        return false
    end
    t41.hasMatchingUserEggsAuthoritative = function(p212)
        if p212.enabled then
            if p212.userStealEnabled and not p212.destroyed then
                if not p212.drScrambleRouteActive and (p212.running or p212.activeTarget ~= nil or p212.tweenRecoveryUid ~= nil) then return true end
                local ok, result = pcall(EggState2.ReadFieldEggs)
                if not ok or type(result) ~= "table" then return p212:hasMatchingUserEggs() end
                local Records = type(result.Records) == "table" and result.Records or result
                for _, Record in pairs(Records) do
                    if type(Record) ~= "table" then continue end
                    if (Record.State == AreaEggs.States.Slot or Record.State == AreaEggs.States.Dropped) and p212:_matchesNormalFilter(Record) then return true end
                    if p212:_isForeignCarryCandidate(Record) then return true end
                end
                return p212:hasMatchingUserEggs()
            end
        end
        return false
    end
    t41._refreshCounts = function(p213)
        local available = 0 local matching = 0
        for _, record in pairs(p213.records) do
            if record.State == AreaEggs.States.Slot then
                available += 1
                if p213:_matches(record) then matching += 1 end
            elseif p213:_isForeignCarryCandidate(record) then matching += 1 end
        end
        p213.stats.available = available p213.stats.matching = matching
        p213:_notify()
    end
    t41._chooseTarget = function(p214)
        local hrp = LocalPlayer2.Character and LocalPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return nil end
        local panelTargetUid = p214.panelTargetUid
        if type(panelTargetUid) == "string" and panelTargetUid ~= "" then
            local v831 = EggState2.ReadFieldEgg(panelTargetUid) or p214.records[panelTargetUid]
            if not (type(v831) ~= "table" or v831.State ~= AreaEggs.States.Slot and v831.State ~= AreaEggs.States.Dropped or p214:isIntentionalGiftDrop(panelTargetUid)) then
                p214.panelTargetUid = nil p214.panelActiveUid = panelTargetUid
                return v831
            end
            p214.panelTargetUid = nil
            if not p214.userStealEnabled and not p214.riftObjectiveEnabled and p214.panelActiveUid == nil then
                task.defer(function() if not p214.destroyed then p214:_reconcileRunningState() end end)
            end
        end
        if p214.userStealEnabled and p214.config.stealFromPlayers then
            local v832, v833, v834
            for _, record in pairs(p214.records) do
                if p214:_isForeignCarryCandidate(record) then
                    local pb = Players2:GetPlayerByUserId(record.CarrierUserId)
                    local hrp2 = pb and pb.Character and pb.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local rr = p214.catalog.resolve(record).rarityRank or 0
                    local dist = hrp2 and (hrp2.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude or math.huge
                    if not v832 or v833 < rr or rr == v833 and dist < v834 then v832 = record v833 = rr v834 = dist end
                end
            end
            if v832 then return v832 end
        end
        if p214.userStealEnabled then
            local v841, v842, v843
            for _, record in pairs(p214.records) do
                if (record.State == AreaEggs.States.Slot or record.State == AreaEggs.States.Dropped) and p214:_matchesNormalFilter(record) then
                    local v846 = p214.catalog.resolve(record)
                    if t38[v846.rarity] then
                        local dist = (record.BottomCFrame.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                        local rr = v846.rarityRank or 0
                        if not v841 or v842 < rr or rr == v842 and dist < v843 then v841 = record v842 = rr v843 = dist end
                    end
                end
            end
            if v841 then return v841 end
        end
        if p214.riftObjectiveEnabled then
            local v849, v850
            for _, record in pairs(p214.records) do
                if (record.State == AreaEggs.States.Slot or record.State == AreaEggs.States.Dropped) and p214:_isRiftObjective(record) then
                    local dist = (record.BottomCFrame.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                    if not v849 or dist < v850 then v849 = record v850 = dist end
                end
            end
            if v849 then return v849 end
        end
        if p214.userStealEnabled then
            local v854, v855, v856
            for _, record in pairs(p214.records) do
                if (record.State == AreaEggs.States.Slot or record.State == AreaEggs.States.Dropped) and p214:_matchesNormalFilter(record) then
                    local dist = (record.BottomCFrame.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                    local rr = p214.catalog.resolve(record).rarityRank or 0
                    if not v854 or v855 < rr or rr == v855 and dist < v856 then v854 = record v855 = rr v856 = dist end
                end
            end
            if v854 then return v854 end
        end
        return nil
    end

    t41.releaseDrScrambleRoute = function(p223, p224)

        local dr = p223.drScrambleRuntime
        p223.drScrambleRouteActive = false p223.safeStarting = false p223.running = false p223.activeTarget = nil
        if dr and type(dr.releaseEventLock) == "function" then dr:releaseEventLock() end
        p223:_cancelTween("Dr. Scramble route released")
        if p224 ~= true and not p223.destroyed and p223.enabled then p223:_scheduleMovementRetry() end
        return true
    end

    t41._finishDrScrambleSafeReturn = function(p229, p230)
        if not p229.drScrambleRouteActive then return end
        p229.drScrambleRouteActive = false p229.safeStarting = false p229.running = false p229.activeTarget = nil
        p229:_releaseOperation()

        p229:_setStatus(p230 and "Safe zone ready for event" or "Retrying event safe zone")

    end
    t41._returnToSafeForDrScramble = function(p231, p232)
        if p231.drScrambleRouteActive or not p232 then return false end
        local v862 = p231:_resolveSafeCFrame(p232)
        if not v862 then return false end
        local tk, v864 = p231.coordinator:tryAcquire("auto_dr_scramble", {})
        if tk then
            p231.operationToken = tk p231.drScrambleRouteActive = true p231.safeStarting = true p231.running = true p231.activeTarget = nil
            p231:_setStatus("Returning to safe zone for event")
            p231:_tweenRoot(v862, function() p231:_finishDrScrambleSafeReturn(true) end, nil, {
                flightProfile = true,
                abort = function() return not p231.drScrambleObjectiveEnabled or not p231:_wallOpen() end,
                onDone = function(p233) if not p233 then p231:_finishDrScrambleSafeReturn(false) end end
            })
            return true
        end
        p231:_setStatus("Dr. Scramble safe zone waiting - " .. tostring(v864 or "busy"))
        p231:_scheduleMovementRetry()
        return false
    end
    t41._startDrScrambleRoute = function(p234, p235)
        if p234.drScrambleRouteActive or type(p235) ~= "table" or typeof(p235.destination) ~= "CFrame" then return false end

        if not tk then
            p234:_setStatus("Dr. Scramble waiting - " .. tostring(v866 or "busy"))
            p234:_scheduleMovementRetry()

        end

            onDone = function(p237) if not p237 then p234:_finishDrScrambleTravel(false, dr, v868) end end
        })
        return true
    end

    t41._triggerInstantForestHit = function(p253, p254)
        if p253.destroyed or not p253.enabled or not p253.waitingGuardHit or p253.baitWaitToken ~= p254 or p253.instantPhase ~= "priming" then return end
        local c = LocalPlayer2.Character
        local hrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local Forest = p253:_findAreaGuardRoot("Forest")
        if not hrp or not Forest then return end
        local primerUid = p253.primerUid
        if not primerUid then return end
        hrp.CFrame = cFrameNew(Forest.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.8, 0))
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        local Collider = Forest.Parent and Forest.Parent:FindFirstChild("Collider") or Forest
        local fti = v694().firetouchinterest or firetouchinterest
        if Collider:IsA("BasePart") and type(fti) == "function" then
            pcall(function() fti(hrp, Collider, 0) fti(hrp, Collider, 1) end)
        end
        if p253.baitWaitToken ~= p254 or not p253.waitingGuardHit then return end
        local ForestStrike = Remotes.GuardPatrol and Remotes.GuardPatrol.ForestStrike
        if ForestStrike then
            pcall(function() ForestStrike:FireServer({ EggUid = primerUid, GuardCFrame = Forest.CFrame }) end)
        end
    end
    t41._choosePrimerEgg = function(p255, p256)
        local __OBJECTS = workspace:FindFirstChild("__OBJECTS")

        local hrp = Guard and Guard:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        local v902, v903
        for _, record in pairs(p255.records) do
            if (record.State == AreaEggs.States.Slot or record.State == AreaEggs.States.Dropped) and record.AreaId == "Forest" and record.Uid ~= p256 then
                local v906 = -(Position and (record.BottomCFrame.Position - Position).Magnitude or LocalPlayer2.Character and LocalPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and (record.BottomCFrame.Position - LocalPlayer2.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude or math.huge) - (p255.catalog.resolve(record).rarityRank or 0) * 100
                if not v902 or v903 < v906 then v902 = record v903 = v906 end
            end
        end
        return v902
    end
    t41._enterBaitWait = function(p257)
        if not p257.baitWaitToken and not p257.destroyed then
            if p257.enabled and p257.instantPhase == "priming" and p257.primerUid then
                local c = LocalPlayer2.Character
                local hrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local Hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if not hrp or not Hum then p257:_finish("Character unavailable") return end
                p257.carryAttemptToken = nil
                if p257.activeTween then pcall(p257.activeTween.Cancel, p257.activeTween) end
                p257:_clearArrivalHold() p257:_stopHoverPad() p257:_setInstantAnchored(false)
                local baitWaitToken = {}
                local primerUid = p257.primerUid
                p257.baitWaitToken = baitWaitToken p257.waitingGuardHit = true
                p257:_setStatus("Waiting for guard hit")
                p257.baitHitConnections = {}
                local u912 = false
                local function v913()
                    if p257.baitWaitToken == baitWaitToken and p257.waitingGuardHit then u912 = true end
                end
                v660(Hum.StateChanged, function(p258, p259)
                    if p259 == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or p259 == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown or p259 == Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding then v913() end
                end, p257.baitHitConnections)
                v660(Hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("PlatformStand"), function() if Hum.PlatformStand then v913() end end, p257.baitHitConnections)
                local Packages = ReplicatedStorage2:FindFirstChild("Packages")
                local Networking = Packages and Packages:FindFirstChild("Networking")
                local RERigSyncRefresh = Networking and Networking:FindFirstChild("RE/RigSync/Refresh")
                if RERigSyncRefresh then pcall(function() v660(RERigSyncRefresh.OnClientEvent, v913, p257.baitHitConnections) end) end
                if p257.antiGuard then p257.antiGuard:setPrimerHitWindow(true) end
                p257.hitTimer = task.defer(function()
                    local v917 = os.clock() + 1.2
                    local n11 = 0
                    local exitTo
                    while true do
                        if p257.baitWaitToken ~= baitWaitToken or not p257.waitingGuardHit or p257.destroyed or not p257.enabled or p257.instantPhase ~= "priming" then exitTo = 1 break end
                        if LocalPlayer2.Character ~= c or not hrp.Parent or not Hum.Parent then exitTo = 2 break end
                        local state = Hum:GetState()
                        if not u912 and not Hum.PlatformStand and not Hum.Sit and state ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll and state ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown and not (hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude > 18) then
                            local now = os.clock()
                            if not (v917 <= now) then
                                if n11 <= now then
                                    n11 = now + 0.06
                                    p257:_triggerInstantForestHit(baitWaitToken)
                                end
                                RunService2.Heartbeat:Wait()
                                continue
                            end
                            exitTo = 1 break
                        end
                        break
                    end
                    if exitTo ~= 1 then
                        if exitTo == 2 then p257:_kbCancelTimer() p257:_finish("Forest bait interrupted") return end
                        p257:_startPrimerTargetHunt() return
                    end
                    if p257.baitWaitToken ~= baitWaitToken then return end
                    p257:_kbCancelTimer() p257.lastAbandonedPrimerUid = primerUid
                    p257.instantPhase = nil p257.primerUid = nil p257.targetUid = nil
                    p257.carry.active = false p257.carry.uid = nil
                    if LocalPlayer2.Character == c and hrp.Parent and Hum.Parent then p257:_teleportRoot(hrp, Hum, p257:_resolveSafeCFrame(hrp)) end
                    p257:_finish("Forest guard hit timed out")
                end)
                return
            end
        end
    end
    t41._findPlayerStealBat = function()
        local c = LocalPlayer2.Character
        local Backpack = LocalPlayer2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
        local function sc(p260)
            for _, child in ipairs(p260 and p260:GetChildren() or {}) do

            end
            return nil
        end
        return sc(c) or sc(Backpack)
    end
    t41._ensurePlayerStealBat = function(p261, p262)
        local v929 = os.clock() + 2.5
        local n12 = 0
        while not p261.destroyed and p261.enabled and p261.playerHuntToken == p262 and os.clock() < v929 do
            local c = LocalPlayer2.Character
            local Hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not c or not Hum or Hum.Health <= 0 then return nil end
            local v933 = p261:_findPlayerStealBat()
            if v933 then
                if v933.Parent ~= c then pcall(Hum.EquipTool, Hum, v933) end
                if v933.Parent == c then
                    task.wait(0.08)
                    if v933.Parent == c then return v933 end
                end
            end
            local now = os.clock()
            if n12 <= now then
                n12 = now + 0.45
                p261:_setStatus("Requesting Field Bat")
                pcall(function() Remotes.Codex.AskWearFieldBat:InvokeServer() end)
            end
            task.wait(0.05)
        end
        return nil
    end

        end

    end
    t41._startPlayerCarryHunt = function(p266, p267)

            end

                local _, v950 = v940(true)
                if not v950 then return nil end
                local now2 = os.clock()

    t41._wallOpen = function(p270)
        if not p270.resetBlocked and not AreaEggResetWall2.IsSealed() then
            if not (os.clock() < p270.gateOpenAt) then
                local Areas = workspace:FindFirstChild("__OBJECTS") and workspace.__OBJECTS:FindFirstChild("Areas")
                local WallStartCollision = Areas and Areas:FindFirstChild("WallStartCollision")
                if WallStartCollision and WallStartCollision:IsA("BasePart") and WallStartCollision.CanCollide then return false end
                return true
            end
        end
        return false
    end

        end

    end

    local function v996(p285)
        local v997 = p285.Size.X * 0.5
        local v998 = p285.Size.Z * 0.5

            huge2 = math.min(huge2, v1005.Z) v1002 = math.max(v1002, v1005.Z)
        end
        return huge, v1000, huge2, v1002
    end
    t41._snapshotZigzagWalls = function()
        local __OBJECTS = workspace:FindFirstChild("__OBJECTS")
        local Build = __OBJECTS and __OBJECTS:FindFirstChild("Build")
        local t44 = {}
        if not Build then return t44 end
        local function v1009(p286)
            local v1010 = string.upper(p286.Name)
            if v1010 == "SIDE_WALLS" then return "high" end
            if v1010 == "SIDE_WALLS2" then return "low" end
            local Parent = p286
            while Parent and Parent ~= Build do
                local v1012 = string.upper(Parent.Name)
                if string.find(v1012, "WALL LEFT", 1, true) then return "low" end
                if string.find(v1012, "WALL RIGHT", 1, true) then return "high" end
                Parent = Parent.Parent
            end
            return nil
        end
        for _, desc in ipairs(Build:GetDescendants()) do
            if desc:IsA("BasePart") and desc.CanCollide then
                local v1015 = v1009(desc)
                if v1015 then
                    local v1016, v1017, v1018, v1019 = v996(desc)
                    if v1017 - v1016 >= 100 then
                        table.insert(t44, { side = v1015, minX = v1016, maxX = v1017, innerZ = v1015 == "low" and v1019 or v1018 })
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return t44
    end
    t41._resolveZigzagBounds = function(p287, p288)
        local v1020 = -math.huge local huge = math.huge
        local v1022 = false local v1023 = false
        for _, zzw in ipairs(p287.zigzagWalls or {}) do
            if zzw.minX <= p288 and p288 <= zzw.maxX then
                if zzw.side == "low" and zzw.innerZ < vector32.Z then v1020 = math.max(v1020, zzw.innerZ) v1022 = true
                elseif zzw.side == "high" and vector32.Z < zzw.innerZ then huge = math.min(huge, zzw.innerZ) v1023 = true end
            end
        end
        if not v1022 or not v1023 then return nil end
        local v1028 = v1020 + 18
        local v1029 = huge - 18
        if v1029 - v1028 < 48 then return nil end
        return v1028, v1029
    end
    t41._buildSafeZigzagWaypoints = function(p289, p290, p291)

    t41._showTreadmillDecoy = function(p294)
        if p294.treadmillDecoy and p294.treadmillDecoy.Parent then return true end
        p294:_hideTreadmillDecoy()
        local c = LocalPlayer2.Character
        if not c then return false end
        local Arch = c.Archivable
        c.Archivable = true
        local ok, td = pcall(c.Clone, c)
        c.Archivable = Arch
        if not ok or not td or typeof(td) ~= "Instance" then return false end
        td.Name = "__FyyTreadmillDecoy"
        for _, desc in ipairs(td:GetDescendants()) do
            if t40[desc.ClassName] or desc:IsA("Trail") then pcall(desc.Destroy, desc)
            elseif desc:IsA("BasePart") then desc.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0 desc.Anchored = true desc.CanCollide = false desc.CanQuery = false desc.CanTouch = false end
        end
        local hrp = td:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local Hum = td:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hrp and Hum then
            pcall(function() Hum.DisplayDistanceType = Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None Hum.HealthDisplayType = Enum.HumanoidHealthDisplayType.AlwaysOff end)
            td.Parent = workspace td:PivotTo(cFrameNew(vector32))
            p294.treadmillDecoy = td
            local pg = LocalPlayer2:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
            if pg then
                for _, desc in ipairs(pg:GetDescendants()) do v1041(desc) end
                p294.treadmillPopupConnection = pg.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(desc) if p294.treadmillDecoy == td then v1041(desc) end end)
            end
            local realTrans = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
            local function v1054(p295)
                if not p295 or not p295:IsA("BasePart") or not p295:IsDescendantOf(c) then return end
                if realTrans[p295] == nil then realTrans[p295] = p295.LocalTransparencyModifier end
                p295.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
            end
            for _, desc in ipairs(c:GetDescendants()) do v1054(desc) v1040(desc) end
            p294.treadmillDecoyRealTransparency = realTrans
            p294.treadmillDecoyCharacterConnection = c.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(desc) if p294.treadmillDecoy == td then pcall(v1054, desc) v1040(desc) end end)
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            if cam then p294.treadmillDecoyCamera = cam.CameraSubject cam.CameraSubject = Hum end
            p294.treadmillDecoyCameraConnection = RunService2.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                if p294.treadmillDecoy ~= td or not td.Parent or not Hum.Parent then return end
                for k in pairs(realTrans) do if k.Parent and k.LocalTransparencyModifier ~= 1 then k.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1 end end
                local cam2 = workspace.CurrentCamera
                if cam2 and cam2.CameraSubject ~= Hum then cam2.CameraSubject = Hum end
            end)
            return true
        end
        td:Destroy()
        return false
    end

    end

    t41._startHoverPad = function(p348)

    t41._waitForGuardReturn = function(p368, p369, p370)
        local c = LocalPlayer2.Character
        local grw = p368.guardReturnWait
        if not grw or grw.uid ~= p369 then
            grw = { uid = p369, deadline = os.clock() + 15, character = c }
            p368.guardReturnWait = grw
        end
        p368.dropRecoveryUid = p369
        p368:_clearArrivalHold(p369)
        if p368.activeTween then pcall(p368.activeTween.Cancel, p368.activeTween) end
        local function v1217(p371, p372)
            if p368.guardReturnWait == grw then p368.guardReturnWait = nil end
            return p371, p372
        end
        while p370() and p368.guardReturnWait == grw do
            if LocalPlayer2.Character ~= grw.character or not c or not c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then return v1217(nil, "Character unavailable") end
            if not p368:_wallOpen() then return v1217(nil, "Wall closed during guard return") end
            if p368:isPrimerEgg(p369) or p368:isIntentionalGiftDrop(p369) then return v1217(nil, "Egg excluded from recovery") end
            local v1218 = EggState2.ReadFieldEgg(p369) or p368.records[p369]
            if v1218 then
                if v1218.State == AreaEggs.States.Claimed then return v1217(nil, "Egg already claimed") end
                if v1218.State == AreaEggs.States.Carried and v1218.CarrierUserId and v1218.CarrierUserId ~= LocalPlayer2.UserId then return v1217(nil, "Egg carried by another player") end
                if v1218.State == AreaEggs.States.GuardCarried then grw.guardSeen = true
                elseif not (v1218.State ~= AreaEggs.States.Slot and (v1218.State ~= AreaEggs.States.Dropped or grw.guardSeen)) then return v1217(v1218) end
            end
            if not (grw.deadline <= os.clock()) then
                p368:_setStatus("Waiting for guard to return egg")
                task.wait(0.03)
                continue
            end
            return v1217(nil, "Guard return timed out")
        end
        return v1217(nil, "Recovery cancelled")
    end
    t41._reStealDroppedEgg = function(p373, p374)
        if type(p374) == "string" and p374 ~= "" then
            if not p373:isPrimerEgg(p374) and not p373:isIntentionalGiftDrop(p374) then
                if p373.tweenRecoveryUid == p374 then

                    p373:_releaseOperation() p373:_scheduleMovementRetry()
                    return

                local v1219 = p373:_claimCarryWorker("re-steal", p374)
                if not v1219 then return end

                p373.dropRecoveryUid = p374
                p373:_setStatus("Re-stealing dropped egg")
                local v1221, v1222 = p373:_waitForGuardReturn(p374, function() return p373:_isCarryWorkerCurrent(v1219) and p373:_isDropRecoveryPending(p374) end)
                if not p373:_isCarryWorkerCurrent(v1219) then return end
                if not p373:_isDropRecoveryPending(p374) then p373:_releaseCarryWorker(v1219) return end
                if not v1221 or not p373:_wallOpen() then
                    p373:_releaseCarryWorker(v1219)
                    p373:_finish(v1222 or "Egg lost - retrying")
                    return
                end
                p373:_cancelTween("Re-stealing dropped egg", true)
                local c = LocalPlayer2.Character
                local hrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local Hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hrp then hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero end
                local v1226 = p373:_resolveEggDestination(v1221, hrp, Hum)
                if not v1226 then p373:_releaseCarryWorker(v1219) p373:_finish("Character unavailable") return end
                p373.running = true p373.activeTarget = v1221
                p373:_setStatus("Teleporting to dropped egg")
                if not p373:_teleportRoot(hrp, Hum, v1226) then p373:_releaseCarryWorker(v1219) p373:_finish("Dropped egg teleport failed") return end
                p373:_setArrivalHold(v1226, v1221.Uid)
                p373:_setStatus("Re-stealing egg")
                if p373:_isCarryWorkerCurrent(v1219) then p373:_requestCarry(v1221, true) end
                return
            end
        end
        p373:_finish("Egg lost - retrying")
    end
    t41._recoverCarryConfirmationTimeout = function(p375, p376, p377, p378)
        if not p375.destroyed and p375.enabled then
            if p375.carryAttemptToken == p376 and not p375:_hasConfirmedCarry(p377) then
                local c = LocalPlayer2.Character
                local hrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local v1229 = hrp and p375:_resolveSafeCFrame(hrp)

                p375.tweenRecoveryUid = p377
                p375.dropRecoveryUid = nil p375.guardReturnWait = nil
                local cw = p375.carry cw.generation += 1
                p375:_invalidateCarryWorker() p375.carryAttemptToken = nil
                p375:_clearArrivalHold(p377)
                if p375.activeTween and p375.activeTween.Cancel then pcall(p375.activeTween.Cancel, p375.activeTween) end
                p375.activeTween = nil p375:_stopMovementProtection()
                p375.running = false p375.activeTarget = nil
                p375.stats.lastResult = p378 and "Carry timeout: " .. p378 or "Carry confirmation timeout"
                if hrp and v1230 then
                    pcall(function()
                        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        hrp.CFrame = v1230
                        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    end)
                    p375:_setStatus(p375.stats.lastResult .. " - teleported safe")
                else p375:_setStatus(p375.stats.lastResult .. " - safe area unavailable") end
                if p375.enabled then p375:_startMovementProtection() end
                p375:_releaseOperation()
                task.delay(0.35, function() if not p375.destroyed and p375.enabled and p375.carryAttemptToken == nil then p375:_schedule() end end)
                return true
            end
        end
        return false
    end

    t41._dropCarriedEggAtFirstArea = function(p382)
        local uid = p382.carry.uid
        if type(uid) ~= "string" or uid == "" or p382:isPrimerEgg(uid) then return false end
        local v1266 = p382:_claimCarryWorker("gift-drop", uid)
        if not v1266 then return false end
        local c = LocalPlayer2.Character
        local hrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local Hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local v1270 = p382:_resolveForestDropCFrame(hrp)
        if not hrp or not Hum or not v1270 then
            p382:_releaseCarryWorker(v1266)
            p382:_finish("Forest drop destination unavailable")
            return false
        end
        if not p382:_wallOpen() then
            p382:_releaseCarryWorker(v1266)
            p382:_finish("Forest drop interrupted by reset")
            return false
        end
        p382.running = true p382:_setStatus("Delivering egg to Forest")
        p382:_tweenRoot(v1270, function()
            if not p382:_isCarryWorkerCurrent(v1266) then return end
            if p382.destroyed or not p382.enabled or not p382.carry.active or p382.carry.uid ~= uid then p382:_releaseCarryWorker(v1266) return end
            p382.intentionalGiftUid = uid
            p382:_setStatus("Dropping egg at Forest")
            task.spawn(function()
                local v1271 = os.clock() + 2
                while p382:_isCarryWorkerCurrent(v1266) and not p382.destroyed and p382.enabled and p382.intentionalGiftUid == uid and p382.carry.active and p382.carry.uid == uid and os.clock() < v1271 do
                    local ok, result = pcall(function() return EggWorld.AskFieldEggDrop:InvokeServer({ Reason = "PlayerRequest" }) end)
                    if not ok then break end
                    if result == true then task.wait(0.03) else task.wait(0.08) end
                end
                if p382.intentionalGiftUid == uid and p382.carry.active and p382.carry.uid == uid then
                    p382.intentionalGiftUid = nil
                    p382:_releaseCarryWorker(v1266)
                    p382:_finish("Forest drop not confirmed")
                elseif p382:_isCarryWorkerCurrent(v1266) then p382:_releaseCarryWorker(v1266) end
            end)
        end, nil, { flightProfile = true, arrivalHoldSeconds = 0 })
        return true
    end
    t41._returnToSafe = function(p383)
        p383:_hideTreadmillDecoy()
        local uid = p383.carry.uid
        if p383:isPrimerEgg(uid) then return end
        if p383.config.dropAtFirstArea then p383:_dropCarriedEggAtFirstArea() return end
        local v1275 = p383:_claimCarryWorker("return", uid)
        if not v1275 then return end
        p383:_clearArrivalHold(uid)
        if not p383:_wallOpen() then p383:_releaseCarryWorker(v1275) p383:_finish("Reset interrupted carry") return end
        local c = LocalPlayer2.Character
        local hrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then p383:_releaseCarryWorker(v1275) p383:_finish("Character unavailable") return end
        if not p383:_hasConfirmedCarry(uid) or p383:_recordShowsForeignCarry(uid) then p383:_releaseCarryWorker(v1275) p383:_finish("Carry not confirmed - retrying") return end
        if p383:_isInSafeArea(hrp) then p383:_finalizeReturn(v1275) return end
        local v1278 = p383:_resolveSafeCFrame(hrp)
        if not v1278 then p383:_releaseCarryWorker(v1275) p383:_finish("Safe destination unavailable") return end
        local v1279 = p383:_buildSafeZigzagWaypoints(hrp, v1278)
        local v1280 = v1279[1]
        if not v1280 then
            p383:_setStatus("Returning to safe area")
            p383:_tweenRoot(v1278, function() if p383:_isCarryWorkerCurrent(v1275) then p383:_finalizeReturn(v1275) end end, nil, { flightProfile = true, arrivalHoldSeconds = 0 })
            return
        end
        local t46 = {}
        for i = 2, #v1279 do table.insert(t46, v1279[i]) end
        table.insert(t46, v1278)
        p383:_setStatus(string.format("Zigzag return - %d turns", #v1279))
        p383:_tweenRoot(v1280, function() if p383:_isCarryWorkerCurrent(v1275) then p383:_finalizeReturn(v1275) end end, nil, { pathWaypoints = t46, flightProfile = true, arrivalHoldSeconds = 0 })
    end
    t41._finalizeReturn = function(p384, p385)
        if not p384:_isCarryWorkerCurrent(p385) then return end
        if not p384.carry.active then
            p384.hasDepositedThisSession = true
            p384:_releaseCarryWorker(p385)
            p384:_finish("Safe return complete")
            return
        end
        p384:_setStatus("Validating safe arrival")
        local now = os.clock()
        local v1284 = os.clock() + 6
        while p384:_isCarryWorkerCurrent(p385) and p384.carry.active and os.clock() < v1284 do
            local hrp = LocalPlayer2.Character and LocalPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not hrp then p384:_releaseCarryWorker(p385) p384:_finish("Character unavailable") return end
            if not p384:_isInSafeArea(hrp) then
                p384:_setStatus("Rollback detected - returning again")
                p384:_releaseCarryWorker(p385)
                task.defer(function() if not p384.destroyed and p384.enabled and p384.carry.active then p384:_returnToSafe() end end)
                return
            end
            if os.clock() - now >= 0.35 then p384:_setStatus("Waiting for deposit") end
            task.wait(0.08)
        end
        if not p384:_isCarryWorkerCurrent(p385) then return end
        if not p384.carry.active then p384:_releaseCarryWorker(p385) p384:_finish("Safe return complete") return end
        p384:_setStatus("Deposit pending - validating again")
        task.defer(function() if p384:_isCarryWorkerCurrent(p385) and p384.carry.active then p384:_finalizeReturn(p385) end end)
    end
    t41._finish = function(p386, p387)
        p386:_invalidateCarryWorker()
        p386.planGeneration += 1
        if p386.panelActiveUid ~= nil then p386.panelActiveUid = nil end
        local v1286 = p386:hasDrPriorityEggsAuthoritative(true)
        local v1287 = p386.riftRuntime and type(p386.riftRuntime.isPlacePending) == "function" and p386.riftRuntime:isPlacePending()
        local v1288 = not v1286 and p386.drScrambleRuntime and type(p386.drScrambleRuntime.isEventWindowActive) == "function" and p386.drScrambleRuntime:isEventWindowActive()
        local v1289 = p386.captureTheEggRuntime and type(p386.captureTheEggRuntime.isEngaged) == "function" and p386.captureTheEggRuntime:isEngaged()
        local v1290 = p386.enabled and not v1287 and not v1288 and not v1289
        p386:_cancelTween(p387, v1290)
        local v1291 = p386 local _setStatus = p386._setStatus
        local v1293 = v1290 and "Searching"
        _setStatus(v1291, v1293 or v1288 and "Yielding to Dr. Scramble" or v1287 and "Yielding to Rift place" or "Idle")
        if p386.enabled then
            task.delay(0.1, function()
                if p386.destroyed then p386:_releaseOperation() return end
                if not (p386.userStealEnabled or p386.riftObjectiveEnabled or p386.drScrambleObjectiveEnabled or p386.panelTargetUid ~= nil or p386.panelActiveUid ~= nil) then p386:_reconcileRunningState() return end
                p386:_schedule()
            end)
        end
    end
    t41._scheduleMovementRetry = function(p388)
        if not p388.movementRetryPending and not p388.destroyed then
            if p388.enabled then
                p388.movementRetryPending = true
                task.delay(0.2, function() p388.movementRetryPending = false if not p388.destroyed and p388.enabled then p388:_schedule() end end)
                return
            end
        end
    end
    t41._schedule = function(p389)
        if p389.destroyed or not p389.enabled or p389.running or p389.safeStarting or p389.resetSnapshotPending then return end
        if not p389.startupSettled then p389:_setStatus("Waiting for filters") return end
        local c = LocalPlayer2.Character
        local hrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local Hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hrp or not Hum then p389:_releaseOperation() p389:_setStatus("Character unavailable") return end
        p389:_startMovementProtection()
        if p389.carry.active and p389.carry.uid ~= nil then
            if p389:isPrimerEgg(p389.carry.uid) then return end
            p389:_returnToSafe()
            return
        end
        if not p389:_wallOpen() then p389:_releaseOperation() p389:_setStatus("Waiting for reset wall") return end
        if p389.riftRuntime and type(p389.riftRuntime.isTradeReady) == "function" then
            if p389.riftRuntime:isTradeReady() then
                p389:_releaseOperation()
                p389:_setStatus("Yielding to Rift trade")
                task.delay(0.5, function() if not p389.destroyed and p389.enabled then p389:_schedule() end end)
                return
            end
        end
        if p389.riftRuntime and type(p389.riftRuntime.isPlacePending) == "function" then
            if p389.riftRuntime:isPlacePending() then
                p389:_releaseOperation()
                p389:_setStatus("Yielding to Rift place")
                task.delay(0.5, function() if not p389.destroyed and p389.enabled then p389:_schedule() end end)
                return
            end
        end
        if p389.captureTheEggRuntime and type(p389.captureTheEggRuntime.isEngaged) == "function" then
            if p389.captureTheEggRuntime:isEngaged() then
                p389:_releaseOperation()
                p389:_setStatus("Yielding to Capture The Egg")
                task.delay(0.5, function() if not p389.destroyed and p389.enabled then p389:_schedule() end end)
                return
            end
        end
        local tweenRecoveryUid = p389.tweenRecoveryUid
        local v1298 = tweenRecoveryUid and (EggState2.ReadFieldEgg(tweenRecoveryUid) or p389.records[tweenRecoveryUid]) or nil
        if tweenRecoveryUid and (type(v1298) ~= "table" or v1298.State ~= AreaEggs.States.Slot and v1298.State ~= AreaEggs.States.Dropped) then
            p389.tweenRecoveryUid = nil tweenRecoveryUid = nil v1298 = nil
        end
        if not v1298 and p389.drScrambleObjectiveEnabled and p389.drScrambleRuntime then
            if p389.drScrambleRuntime:isEventWindowActive() and not p389:hasDrPriorityEggsAuthoritative() then
                if p389.drScrambleNeedsSafeReturn then
                    if not p389:_isInSafeArea(hrp) then p389:_returnToSafeForDrScramble(hrp) return end
                    p389.drScrambleNeedsSafeReturn = false
                end
                local v1299 = p389.drScrambleRuntime:getEventRoute()
                if v1299 then p389:_startDrScrambleRoute(v1299)
                else p389:_releaseOperation() p389:_setStatus("Waiting for event robot replication") p389:_scheduleMovementRetry() end
                return
            end
        end
        v1298 = v1298 or p389:_chooseTarget()
        if not v1298 then

    t49.startDefend = function(p458, p459)

        if zeroinConfig.IsFusing then return end

            local db = p458.defenseBat
            p458.defenseBat = nil

        end)
    end

        end)

                end))
            end
        end)
    end
    t49.destroy = function(p465)

        for _, c in ipairs(p465.connections) do pcall(c.Disconnect, c) end

    t53._run = function(p504, p505)

            local v1568, v1569 = p504:_askState()
            if not v1568 then p504:_setStatus("Rift state: " .. tostring(v1569)) task.wait(1)

                    else

                        p504.stats.ready = #Req - #v1574
                        p504.stats.missing = #v1574 > 0 and table.concat(v1574, ", ") or "None"

                        local tr = #v1574 == 0
                        p504.tradeReady = tr
                        p504.eggRuntime:setRiftObjective(not tr and #v1575 > 0, v1575)
                        p504.eggLifecycle:setRiftObjective(not tr, Req)
                        if tr then
                            p504.eggRuntime:setRiftObjective(false, {})
                            local v1578, v1579 = p504:_trade(Req, v1573, p505)
                            p504.tradeReady = false
                            p504:_setStatus(v1578 and "Rift trade complete" or "Rift trade: " .. tostring(v1579))
                        elseif #v1575 > 0 then
                            local v1580 = v1575[1]
                            if p504:_isCategoryOnField(v1580) then p504:_setStatus("Stealing: " .. tostring(v1580) .. " egg")
                            else p504:_setStatus("Waiting for " .. tostring(v1580) .. " to spawn") end
                        else p504:_setStatus("Waiting for eggs to hatch") end
                        p504:_emit()
                        task.wait(0.5)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        p504.running = false
    end
    t53._schedule = function(p506)
        if p506.destroyed or not p506.enabled or p506.running then return end
        local generation = p506.generation
        task.defer(function()
            local ok, result = pcall(p506._run, p506, generation)
            if not ok and not p506.destroyed and p506.enabled and generation == p506.generation then
                p506.running = false
                p506:_setStatus("Rift error: " .. tostring(result))
                task.delay(2, function() if not p506.destroyed and p506.enabled and generation == p506.generation then p506:_schedule() end end)
            end
        end)
    end
    t53.setEnabled = function(p507, p508)
        p507.enabled = p508 == true p507.generation += 1
        if p507.enabled then p507:_setStatus("Reading Rift requirements") p507:_schedule()
        else
            p507.tradeReady = false
            p507.eggRuntime:setRiftObjective(false, {})
            p507.eggLifecycle:setRiftObjective(false, {})
            p507.coordinator:clearProtected("auto_the_rift")
            p507.protectedUids = {}
            p507:_setStatus("Disabled")
        end
    end
    t53.destroy = function(p509)
        if p509.destroyed then return end
        p509.destroyed = true p509.enabled = false p509.tradeReady = false p509.generation += 1
        p509.eggRuntime:setRiftObjective(false, {})
        p509.eggLifecycle:setRiftObjective(false, {})
        p509.coordinator:clearProtected("auto_the_rift")
        for _, r in ipairs(p509.resources) do
            pcall(function()
                if typeof(r) == "RBXScriptConnection" then r:Disconnect()
                elseif type(r) == "function" then r()
                elseif type(r) == "table" and type(r.Disconnect) == "function" then r:Disconnect() end
            end)
        end
        table.clear(p509.resources) table.clear(p509.listeners)
    end
    return t53
end)()

            end)

        end
        return false
    end
    t70._purchaseCount = function(p558, p559, p560)
        local State = type(p559) == "table" and p559.State or nil
        local SP = type(State) == "table" and State.ShopPurchases or nil

    end
    t70._selectedShopOffer = function(p561, p562)
        local t75 = {}
        for _, item in ipairs(p561.shopItems) do
            t75[item] = true
            local v1734 = t69[item]
            if v1734 then t75[v1734] = true end

        local Shop = type(p562 and p562.Shop) == "table" and p562.Shop or {}
        for _, v in ipairs(Shop) do

            local sa = p561.shopAmount
            local v1741 = p561:_purchaseCount(p562, v)
            local v1742 = tonumber(v.PurchaseLimit)
            if not (v1739 < sa) then continue end
            if not v1742 then return v end
            if v1741 + (tonumber(v.Quantity) or 1) <= v1742 then return v end
        end
        return nil
    end
    t70._shopTick = function(p563)
        local now = os.clock()
        if now < p563.nextShopCycleAt then p563.shopStatus = string.format("Next purchase cycle in %ds", math.ceil(p563.nextShopCycleAt - now)) return end
        local v1744 = p563:_snapshot()
        if type(v1744) ~= "table" or v1744.Ready ~= true or v1744.Enabled ~= true or v1744.WorldReady ~= true then p563.shopStatus = "Event inactive" return end
        if tonumber(v1744.EventEndsAt) and v1744.EventEndsAt <= Workspace2:GetServerTimeNow() then p563.shopStatus = "Event inactive" return end
        if type(p563.shopItems) ~= "table" or #p563.shopItems == 0 then p563.shopStatus = "No items selected" return end
        local v1745 = p563:_selectedShopOffer(v1744)
        if not v1745 then
            table.clear(p563.shopPurchases) p563.lastShopSignature = nil
            p563.nextShopCycleAt = now + p563.shopInterval
            p563.shopStatus = string.format("Selected offers unavailable or capped - retry in %ds", p563.shopInterval)
            return
        end
        local State = type(v1744.State) == "table" and v1744.State or nil
        if not State then p563.shopStatus = "Shop state unavailable" return end
        if type(v1745.Quote) ~= "string" or v1745.Quote == "" then p563.shopStatus = "Offer quote unavailable" return end
        if not Scramble or not Scramble.Request then p563.shopStatus = "Official shop remote unavailable" return end
        local v1747 = tonumber(State.Samples) or 0
        if v1747 < (tonumber(v1745.Price) or math.huge) then
            p563.nextShopCycleAt = now + p563.shopInterval
            p563.shopStatus = "Not enough Samples for " .. tostring(v1745.Label)
            return
        end
        local v1748 = p563:_purchaseCount(v1744, v1745)
        local t76 = {}
        local v1751 = tostring(v1744.ShopPeriod)
        local v1752 = tostring(v1745.Id)
        local v1753 = tostring(State.ShopSequence)
        local v1754 = tostring(v1747)
        local v1755 = tostring(v1748)
        local v1757 = p563.shopPurchases[v1745.Id] or 0
        do
            local values = table.pack(v1751, v1752, v1753, v1754, v1755, tostring(v1757))
            table.move(values, 1, values.n, 1, t76)
        end
        local lastShopSignature = table.concat(t76, ":")
        if p563.lastShopSignature == lastShopSignature or now - p563.lastShopRequestAt < 1.5 then return end
        p563.lastShopRequestAt = now p563.lastShopSignature = lastShopSignature
        p563.shopStatus = "Buying " .. tostring(v1745.Label)
        local ok, result = pcall(function() return Scramble.Request:InvokeServer("Shop", v1745.Id, { Quote = v1745.Quote, Sequence = math.max(tonumber(State.ShopSequence) or 0, 0) }) end)
        if ok and result ~= false then
            if type(result) == "table" and type(result.Snapshot) == "table" then p563.context = result.Snapshot end
            p563.shopPurchases[v1745.Id] = (p563.shopPurchases[v1745.Id] or 0) + 1
            p563.shopStatus = "Bought " .. tostring(v1745.Label)
        else
            p563.shopStatus = "Purchase failed: " .. tostring(type(result) == "table" and result.Reason or result)
        end
    end
    t70.setEnabled = function(p564, p565)
        p564.enabled = p565 == true p564.generation += 1
        if p564.eggRuntime and type(p564.eggRuntime.setDrScrambleObjective) == "function" then p564.eggRuntime:setDrScrambleObjective(p564.enabled) end

    end
    t70.setShopItems = function(p568, p569)

    t70.setShopInterval = function(p572, p573)

        p572.lastShopSignature = nil p572.nextShopCycleAt = 0
        return p572.shopInterval
    end
    t70.isFightPending = function(p574)
        local enabled = not p574.destroyed and p574.enabled and p574:_eventWindowActive()
        return enabled and p574:_nearestRoute() ~= nil
    end
    t70.isMovementBusy = function(p575) return p575.locked or p575.eggRuntime and p575.eggRuntime.drScrambleRouteActive == true end
    t70.getSnapshot = function(p576)
        local v1763 = p576:_resolveClientContext()
        local st = Workspace2:GetServerTimeNow()
        local Window = type(v1763) == "table" and v1763.Window or nil
        local v1766 = p576:_eventWindowActive(v1763)
        local v1767 = v1766
        if v1767 then v1767 = math.max(tonumber(Workspace2:GetAttribute("ScrambleOutbreakEndsAt")) or 0, type(Window) == "table" and tonumber(Window.EndsAt) or 0) end
        v1767 = v1767 or type(Window) == "table" and (tonumber(Window.NextAt) or tonumber(Window.StartsAt)) or 0
        if v1767 <= 0 then v1767 = (math.floor(st / 1800) + 1) * 1800 end
        local v1768 = p576:_nearestRoute()
        local t77 = {
            enabled = p576.enabled, active = v1766, status = p576.stats.status,
            targetTier = v1768 and (t68[v1768.tier] or v1768.tier) or nil,
            health = v1768 and v1768.health or 0, maxHealth = v1768 and v1768.maxHealth or 0
        }
        t77.secondsLeft = math.max(0, v1767 - st)
        t77.shopStatus = p576.shopStatus
        t77.shopAmount = p576.shopAmount
        t77.shopInterval = p576.shopInterval
        t77.samples = tonumber(type(v1763) == "table" and v1763.State and v1763.State.Samples) or 0
        t77.shopItemCount = type(p576.shopItems) == "table" and #p576.shopItems or 0
        return t77
    end
    t70._ensureWorker = function(p577)
        if p577.worker then return end
        p577.worker = task.spawn(function()
            while not p577.destroyed do
                local v1770 = p577:_resolveClientContext()
                if p577.autoBuy then
                    local ok, result = xpcall(p577._shopTick, debug.traceback, p577)
                    if not ok then warn("[Outlaw][ScrambleShop] " .. tostring(result)) p577.shopStatus = "Shop runtime error" end
                end
                if p577.enabled then p577:_hasPriorityEggs(true) end
                if not p577.enabled then p577:_releaseLock()
                elseif p577:_hasPriorityEggs() then
                    p577:_releaseLock()
                    if p577.eggRuntime and type(p577.eggRuntime.releaseDrScrambleRoute) == "function" then p577.eggRuntime:releaseDrScrambleRoute() end
                    p577.stats.status = "Prioritizing filtered egg"
                elseif not p577:_eventWindowActive(v1770) then
                    p577:_releaseLock() p577.stickyTargetKey = nil
                    if p577.eggRuntime and type(p577.eggRuntime.releaseDrScrambleRoute) == "function" then p577.eggRuntime:releaseDrScrambleRoute() end
                    p577.stats.status = "Waiting for outbreak window"
                elseif not p577:_nearestRoute() then p577:_releaseLock() p577.stats.status = "Waiting for selected drone"
                elseif not p577.locked then p577.stats.status = "Ready for Auto Steal route" end
                genv.OutlawScrambleStatus = p577:getSnapshot()
                task.wait((p577.enabled or p577.autoBuy) and 0.15 or 1)
            end
            p577.worker = nil
        end)
    end
    t70.destroy = function(p578)
        if p578.destroyed then return end
        p578.destroyed = true p578.enabled = false p578.autoBuy = false p578.generation += 1
        if p578.eggRuntime and type(p578.eggRuntime.setDrScrambleObjective) == "function" then p578.eggRuntime:setDrScrambleObjective(false) end
        p578:_releaseLock()
        for _, c in ipairs(p578.connections) do pcall(c.Disconnect, c) end
        table.clear(p578.connections)
        if p578.worker then pcall(task.cancel, p578.worker) p578.worker = nil end
    end
    return t70
end)()

    t79._validateGroup = function(p608, p609)

            end

        if n29 - p608.config.keepPerCategory < 3 then return false, "Keep Copies prevents selected group" end
        for _, entry in ipairs(p609.entries) do
            local v1867 = t86[entry.uid]
            if not v1867 or v1867.category ~= p609.category or not v1867.canFuse or v1867.favorite or v1867.inFuse or v1867.equipped or v1867.placed or not p608:_rarityMatches(v1867) or (p608.config.priority == "Lightest" or p608.config.priority == "Heaviest") and not v1867.weightKnown or p608.config.maxWeightKg > 0 and (not v1867.weightKnown or p608.config.maxWeightKg < v1867.weightKg) then
                return false, "Selected pets changed before fuse"
            end
            if p608.coordinator:isCategoryProtected(v1867.category, "auto_fuse") then return false, "Selected pets changed before fuse" end
            if p608.coordinator:isReserved(v1867.uid, "auto_fuse") then return false, "Selected pets changed before fuse" end
        end
        return true
    end
    t79._waitForTreadmill = function(p610)
        if not p610.treadmillManager then return true end
        if type(p610.treadmillManager._syncActiveTreadmillSnapshot) == "function" then p610.treadmillManager:_syncActiveTreadmillSnapshot() end
        if not p610.treadmillManager:isEngaged() then return true end
        if type(p610.treadmillManager._cancelIdle) == "function" then p610.treadmillManager:_cancelIdle(true) end
        local v1868 = os.clock() + 5
        while p610.treadmillManager:isEngaged() and os.clock() < v1868 do
            if type(p610.treadmillManager._requestUnequip) == "function" then p610.treadmillManager:_requestUnequip() end
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
        return not p610.treadmillManager:isEngaged()
    end
    t79._shouldAbort = function(p611, p612, p613)
        if p611.destroyed or p612 ~= p611.generation or not p613 and not p611.config.enabled then return true end
        if p611.captureRuntime and type(p611.captureRuntime.isEngaged) == "function" and p611.captureRuntime:isEngaged() then return true end
        if p611.travel and type(p611.travel.hasPendingPreEventSteal) == "function" and p611.travel:hasPendingPreEventSteal() then return true end
        local scr = genv.OutlawScrambleRuntime
        if scr and type(scr.isFightPending) == "function" and scr:isFightPending() then return true end
        local rt = genv.OutlawRiftRuntime
        return rt and type(rt.isTradeReady) == "function" and rt:isTradeReady() or false
    end
    t79._travelToMachine = function(p614, p615, p616)
        local __OBJECTS = Workspace2:FindFirstChild("__OBJECTS")
        local Machines = __OBJECTS and __OBJECTS:FindFirstChild("Machines")
        local FM = Machines and Machines:FindFirstChild("FuseMachine")
        local M = FM and FM:FindFirstChild("Machine")
        if not M or not M:IsA("BasePart") then return false, "Fuse machine unavailable" end
        local c = LocalPlayer2.Character
        local hrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local Hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hrp or not Hum or Hum.Health <= 0 then return false, "Character unavailable" end
        local Attachment = M:FindFirstChildOfClass("Attachment")
        local WP = Attachment and Attachment.WorldPosition or M.Position
        local v1880 = WP - M.Position
        local v32 = Vector3.new(v1880.X, 0, v1880.Z)
        local Unit = v32.Magnitude > 0.1 and v32.Unit or -M.CFrame.LookVector
        local v1883 = hrp.Size.Y * 0.5
        local v1884 = Hum.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 and math.max(Hum.HipHeight, 0.5)
        local v1885 = Vector3.new(WP.X, M.Position.Y - M.Size.Y * 0.5 + (v1883 + (v1884 or 2)), WP.Z) + Unit * 4
        local cFrame = lookAt(v1885, Vector3.new(WP.X, v1885.Y, WP.Z))
        if (hrp.Position - cFrame.Position).Magnitude <= 7 then return true end
        if not p614.travel then return false, "Shared travel unavailable" end
        if p614.travel:travelAndWait(cFrame, { abort = function() return p614:_shouldAbort(p615, p616) end }) then return true end
        return false, "Travel to fuse machine interrupted"
    end
    t79._rollback = function(p617, p618)
        local EP = v1803("EjectPet")
        if not EP then return end
        for _, v in ipairs(p618) do pcall(EP.InvokeServer, EP, v.uid) end
    end
    t79._clearStuckMachine = function(p619, p620)

    end
    t79._finishPendingFusion = function(p621)
        local v1900 = Save.Get()

                if v1907 ~= nil and v1906.FusionLocked ~= true and v1907 <= Workspace2:GetServerTimeNow() then v1902 = true break end

            end

        local v1908, v1909 = p621:_finishReveal()
        if not v1908 then return true, "Finish reveal failed: " .. tostring(v1909) end
        p621:_setStatus("Recovered previous fuse reward")

    end
    t79._finishReveal = function()
        local FR = v1803("FinishReveal")

    end
    t79._run = function(p622, p623, p624)
        if p622.running or p622.destroyed then return end
        p622.running = true zeroinConfig.IsFusing = true
        local v1914 = false
        while not p622.destroyed and p623 == p622.generation and (p622.config.enabled or p624) do
            local v1915, v1916 = p622:_finishPendingFusion()

            if v1915 then

            else

                if not ok2 then ok2 = not p622.captureRuntime:isEngaged() end

                local tk, v1928 = p622.coordinator:tryAcquire("auto_fuse", t88)

                    if u1929 then return end

                    local CB = v1803("ConfirmBriefing")
                    if CB then CB:InvokeServer() end
                end)

                local v1952 = tonumber(v1950 and v1950.FusionDuration) or 0
                local v1954
                if v1951 then v1954 = math.max(30, v1951 - Workspace2:GetServerTimeNow() + 8)
                elseif v1952 > 0 then v1954 = math.max(30, v1952 + 8)

                task.wait(0.5)

            end
            break
        end
        p622.running = false zeroinConfig.IsFusing = false
        p622:_emit()

            p622.rescheduleRequested = false p622:_scheduleRetry()
        end
    end
    t79._scheduleRetry = function(p625)
        if p625.retryScheduled or p625.destroyed or not p625.config.enabled then return end
        p625.retryScheduled = true
        local gen = p625.generation
        task.delay(1, function()
            p625.retryScheduled = false
            if not p625.destroyed and p625.config.enabled and gen == p625.generation then p625:_schedule() end
        end)
    end
    t79._schedule = function(p626, p627)
        if not p626.startupRecoveryPending then
            if not p626.destroyed and not p626.running and (p626.config.enabled or p627) then
                local gen = p626.generation
                task.defer(function()
                    local ok, result = xpcall(p626._run, debug.traceback, p626, gen, p627 == true)
                    if not ok then
                        p626.running = false zeroinConfig.IsFusing = false
                        p626.coordinator:forceRelease("auto_fuse")
                        p626:_setStatus("Fuse runtime error")
                        warn("[Outlaw][AutoFuse] " .. tostring(result))
                        p626:_scheduleRetry()
                    end
                end)
                return
            end
            return
        end
        p626.rescheduleRequested = p626.config.enabled or p627 == true
        p626.pendingSinglePass = p626.pendingSinglePass or p627 == true
    end
    t79.setEnabled = function(p628, p629)
        p628.config.enabled = p629 == true p628.generation += 1
        zeroinConfig.AutoFuse = p628.config.enabled
        if p628.config.enabled then
            if p628.running then p628.rescheduleRequested = true end
            p628:_setStatus("Scanning pets") p628:_schedule()
        else
            p628.rescheduleRequested = false
            zeroinConfig.IsFusing = p628.running
            p628:_setStatus("Disabled")
        end
    end

        p630.manualConfirmation = nil p630.generation += 1 p630:_emit()

    end

        p632.manualConfirmation = nil p632.generation += 1 p632:_emit()
        if p632.running then p632.rescheduleRequested = true else p632:_schedule() end
        return table.clone(p632.config.rarities)
    end
    t79.setKeepPerCategory = function(p634, p635)

    end
    t79.setMaxWeightKg = function(p636, p637)
        p636.config.maxWeightKg = math.max(tonumber(p637) or 0, 0)

    end
    t79.fuseOnce = function(p638)
        if p638.destroyed or p638.running then return false, "Auto Fuse is busy" end
        p638.generation += 1 p638:_setStatus("Manual fuse requested")
        p638:_schedule(true)
        return true
    end
    t79.requestFuseOnce = function(p639)
        if p639.destroyed or p639.running then return false, "Auto Fuse is busy" end
        local v1976, v1977 = p639:_selectGroup()
        if not v1976 then
            p639.manualConfirmation = nil p639:_setStatus(v1977 or "No eligible fuse group")
            return false, p639.stats.status
        end
        local t91 = { v1976.category }
        for _, e in ipairs(v1976.entries) do t91[#t91 + 1] = e.uid end
        local v1981 = table.concat(t91, ":")
        local mc = p639.manualConfirmation
        if mc and not (mc.signature ~= v1981 or mc.expiresAt < os.clock()) then
            p639.manualConfirmation = nil
            return p639:fuseOnce()
        end
        p639.manualConfirmation = { signature = v1981, expiresAt = os.clock() + 8 }
        p639:_setStatus("Ready: " .. v1976.displayName .. " x3 - press again to confirm")
        return false, "Press again within 8 seconds to confirm"
    end
    t79.preview = function(p640)
        local v1983, v1984 = p640:_selectGroup()
        p640:_emit()
        if v1983 then return { category = v1983.category, displayName = v1983.displayName, uids = { v1983.entries[1].uid, v1983.entries[2].uid, v1983.entries[3].uid } }, nil end

    end
    t79.getSnapshot = function(p641) return p641:_snapshot() end
    t79.destroy = function(p642)
        if p642.destroyed then return end
        p642.destroyed = true p642.config.enabled = false p642.generation += 1
        zeroinConfig.IsFusing = false
        p642.coordinator:forceRelease("auto_fuse")
        for _, r in ipairs(p642.resources) do v1791(r) end
        table.clear(p642.resources) table.clear(p642.listeners)
    end
    return t79
end)()
