    end
    t93._eligibleTargets = function(p664)
        local v2039 = Save.Get()
        local t97 = {}
        for k, v in pairs(v2039 and v2039.EggInventory or {}) do
            if type(k) == "string" and type(v) == "table" and v.Placement ~= nil and type(v.AssetCategory) == "string" then
                local v2045 = Assets.Directory[v.AssetCategory]
                local DisplayName = v2045 and v2045.Rarity and (v2045.Rarity.DisplayName or v2045.Rarity._id) or "Unknown"
                local DisplayName2 = v2045 and (v2045.DisplayName or v2045._id) or v.AssetCategory
                local v2048 = p664:_rate(v, v2039)
                local v2049 = table.find(p664.config.rarities, "All") ~= nil or table.find(p664.config.rarities, DisplayName) ~= nil
                local v2050 = #p664.config.names == 0 or table.find(p664.config.names, DisplayName2) ~= nil
                local v2051 = v2048 ~= nil and v2048 >= p664.config.minValuePerSec
                local v2052 = table.find(type(v.Mutations) == "table" and v.Mutations or {}, p664.config.mutationType) ~= nil or v.BaseMutation == p664.config.mutationType
                if v2049 and v2050 and v2051 and not v2052 then
                    if not p664.coordinator:isReserved(k, "auto_mutate") then
                        t97[#t97 + 1] = { uid = k, egg = v, category = v.AssetCategory, name = DisplayName2, rarity = DisplayName, rate = v2048 }
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        table.sort(t97, function(a, b)
            if a.rate == b.rate then return a.uid < b.uid end
            return a.rate > b.rate
        end)
        p664.stats.eligible = #t97
        p664.stats.target = t97[1] and t97[1].name or nil
        return t97
    end
    t93._noTargetReason = function(p667)
        local v2053 = Save.Get()
        local t98, t99, t100 = {}, {}, {}
        for _, v in pairs(v2053 and v2053.EggInventory or {}) do
            if type(v) == "table" and v.Placement ~= nil and type(v.AssetCategory) == "string" then
                local v2061 = Assets.Directory[v.AssetCategory]
                local DisplayName = v2061 and (v2061.DisplayName or v2061._id) or v.AssetCategory
                t98[DisplayName] = true
                if #p667.config.names == 0 or table.find(p667.config.names, DisplayName) then
                    t99[DisplayName] = true
                    if table.find(type(v.Mutations) == "table" and v.Mutations or {}, p667.config.mutationType) or v.BaseMutation == p667.config.mutationType then t100[DisplayName] = true end
                end
            end
        end
        if #p667.config.names > 0 then
            local t101, t102 = {}, {}
            for _, name in ipairs(p667.config.names) do
                if not t98[name] then t101[#t101 + 1] = name
                elseif t100[name] then t102[#t102 + 1] = name end
            end
            if #t102 == #p667.config.names then return table.concat(t102, ", ") .. " already has " .. p667.config.mutationType .. " mutation" end
            if #t101 == #p667.config.names then return "Selected egg is no longer placed" end
        end
        if next(t99) and next(t100) then return "Matching placed eggs already have " .. p667.config.mutationType .. " mutation" end
        if p667.config.minValuePerSec > 0 then return "No placed egg meets the minimum value" end
        return "No placed egg matches the rarity or name filters"
    end
    t93._findModel = function(p668, p669)
        local PER = Workspace2:FindFirstChild("PlacedEggRenders")
        if not PER then return nil end
        local v2068 = PER:FindFirstChild(string.format("%d_%s", LocalPlayer2.UserId, p669))
        if v2068 and v2068:IsA("Model") then return v2068 end
        for _, child in ipairs(PER:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("Model") and child.Name:find(p669, 1, true) then return child end
        end
        return nil
    end
    t93._projectGround = function(p670, p671)
        local c = LocalPlayer2.Character
        local hrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local Hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hrp or not Hum then return nil end
        local rp = RaycastParams.new()
        rp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        local filter = { c }
        local PER = Workspace2:FindFirstChild("PlacedEggRenders")
        if PER then filter[#filter + 1] = PER end
        rp.FilterDescendantsInstances = filter rp.IgnoreWater = false
        local hit = Workspace2:Raycast(Vector3.new(p671.X, math.max(p671.Y, hrp.Position.Y) + 40, p671.Z), Vector3.new(0, -160, 0), rp)
        if hit then
            return Vector3.new(p671.X, hit.Position.Y + (hrp.Size.Y * 0.5 + (Hum.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 and math.max(Hum.HipHeight, 0.5) or 2)) + 0.05, p671.Z)
        end
        return nil
    end
    t93._destination = function(p672, p673)
        local c = LocalPlayer2.Character
        local hrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return nil end
        local P = p673:GetPivot().Position
        local v32 = Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X - P.X, 0, hrp.Position.Z - P.Z)
        local v2082 = p672:_projectGround(P + (v32.Magnitude > 0.001 and v32.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)) * 4.5)
        if v2082 then return lookAt(v2082, Vector3.new(P.X, v2082.Y, P.Z)) end
        return nil
    end
    t93._shouldAbort = function(p674, p675, p676)
        if not p674.destroyed and p675 == p674.generation then
            if p676 or p674.config.enabled then
                if p674.captureRuntime and type(p674.captureRuntime.isEngaged) == "function" and p674.captureRuntime:isEngaged() then return true end
                if p674.travel and type(p674.travel.hasPendingPreEventSteal) == "function" and p674.travel:hasPendingPreEventSteal() then return true end
                local scr = genv.OutlawScrambleRuntime
                if scr and type(scr.isFightPending) == "function" and scr:isFightPending() then return true end
                local rt = genv.OutlawRiftRuntime
                return rt and type(rt.isTradeReady) == "function" and rt:isTradeReady() or false
            end
        end
        return true
    end
    t93._applyTarget = function(p677, p678, p679, p680)
        local v2085 = p677:_findModel(p678.uid)
        if not v2085 then p677:_setStatus("Waiting for placed egg model") return false end
        if not p677:_ensureEquipped() then p677:_setStatus("Unable to equip " .. p677.config.mutationType .. " consumable") return false end
        if p677.treadmillManager and type(p677.treadmillManager._syncActiveTreadmillSnapshot) == "function" then p677.treadmillManager:_syncActiveTreadmillSnapshot() end
        if p677.treadmillManager and p677.treadmillManager:isEngaged() then
            if type(p677.treadmillManager._cancelIdle) == "function" then p677.treadmillManager:_cancelIdle(true) end
            if type(p677.treadmillManager._requestUnequip) == "function" then p677.treadmillManager:_requestUnequip() end
        end
        local v2086 = p677:_destination(v2085)
        local hrp = LocalPlayer2.Character and LocalPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if v2086 then
            if hrp then
                if (hrp.Position - v2086.Position).Magnitude > 8 then
                    p677:_setStatus("Moving to " .. p678.name)
                    if not p677.travel:travelAndWait(v2086, {
                        groundedProfile = true,
                        projectGroundPosition = function(p681) return p677:_projectGround(p681) end,
                        abort = function() return p677:_shouldAbort(p679, p680) end
                    }) or p677:_shouldAbort(p679, p680) then
                        p677:_setStatus("Mutation travel interrupted")
                        return false
                    end
                end
                local v2088 = Save.Get()
                local EI = v2088 and v2088.EggInventory and v2088.EggInventory[p678.uid]
                local v2090 = p677:_eligibleTargets()
                local v2091 = false
                for _, v in ipairs(v2090) do if v.uid == p678.uid then v2091 = true break end end
                if not (EI and EI.Placement ~= nil and p677:getConsumableCount() > 0 and v2091) then
                    p677:_setStatus("Target changed before mutation")
                    return false
                end
                if not BossMastery or not BossMastery.AskUseMutationConsumable then p677:_setStatus("Mutation remote unavailable") return false end
                p677:_setStatus("Applying " .. p677.config.mutationType .. " to " .. p678.name)
                local ok, result = pcall(BossMastery.AskUseMutationConsumable.InvokeServer, BossMastery.AskUseMutationConsumable, p678.uid)
                if not ok or type(result) ~= "table" or not result.Success then p677:_setStatus("Server rejected mutation request") return false end
                if result.Mutated then
                    p677.stats.lastMutated = p678.name
                    p677.successHoldUntil = os.clock() + 5
                    p677:_setStatus("Mutation applied to " .. p678.name)
                else p677:_setStatus("Mutation missed; retrying") end
                return true
            end
        end
        p677:_setStatus("Placed egg position unavailable")
        return false
    end
    t93._run = function(p682, p683, p684)
        if p682.running or p682.destroyed then return end
        p682.running = true
        local v2096 = p682:_eligibleTargets()
        p682.stats.consumables = p682:getConsumableCount()
        local v2097 = v2096[1]
        if v2097 or not (os.clock() < p682.successHoldUntil) then
            if not v2097 then p682:_setStatus(p682:_noTargetReason())
            elseif p682.stats.consumables <= 0 then p682:_setStatus("No " .. p682.config.mutationType .. " consumables left")
            elseif p682:_shouldAbort(p683, p684) then
                if p683 == p682.generation then p682:_setStatus("Waiting for another automation") end
            else
                local tk, v2099 = p682.coordinator:tryAcquire("auto_mutate", { v2097.uid })
                if tk then
                    p682:_applyTarget(v2097, p683, p684)
                    p682.coordinator:release(tk)
                else p682:_setStatus(v2099 or "Waiting for another automation") end
            end
        end
        p682.running = false
        if p683 == p682.generation then p682:_emit() end
        if p682.config.enabled and not p682.destroyed and not p684 then
            task.delay(0.9, function() p682:_schedule() end)
        end
    end
    t93._schedule = function(p685, p686)
        if p685.destroyed or p685.running or not p685.config.enabled and not p686 then return end
        local gen = p685.generation
        task.defer(function()
            local ok, result = xpcall(p685._run, debug.traceback, p685, gen, p686 == true)
            if not ok then
                p685.running = false
                p685.coordinator:forceRelease("auto_mutate")
                p685:_setStatus("Mutation runtime error")
                warn("[Outlaw][AutoMutate] " .. tostring(result))
                if p685.config.enabled and not p685.destroyed then task.delay(0.9, function() p685:_schedule() end) end
            end
        end)
    end
    t93.setEnabled = function(p687, p688)
        p687.config.enabled = p688 == true
        zeroinConfig.AutoMutate = p687.config.enabled
        p687.generation += 1
        p687:_setStatus(p687.config.enabled and "Scanning placed eggs" or "Disabled")
        if p687.config.enabled then p687:_schedule()
        else p687.coordinator:forceRelease("auto_mutate") end
    end
    t93.setMutationType = function(p689, p690)
        if table.find(t92, p690) then p689.config.mutationType = p690 end
        zeroinConfig.AutoMutateType = p689.config.mutationType
        p689.manualConfirmation = nil p689.successHoldUntil = 0 p689.generation += 1
        p689:_emit() p689:_schedule()
        return p689.config.mutationType
    end
    t93.setRarities = function(p691, p692)
        local t103 = {}
        for _, v in ipairs(type(p692) == "table" and p692 or { p692 }) do
            if type(v) == "string" and v ~= "" and not table.find(t103, v) then t103[#t103 + 1] = v end
        end
        p691.config.rarities = table.find(t103, "All") and { "All" } or #t103 > 0 and t103 or { "All" }
        zeroinConfig.AutoMutateRarities = table.clone(p691.config.rarities)
        p691.manualConfirmation = nil p691.successHoldUntil = 0 p691.generation += 1
        p691:_emit() p691:_schedule()
        return table.clone(p691.config.rarities)
    end
    t93.setNames = function(p693, p694)
        local v2108 = p693:getPlacedEggNames()
        local names = {}
        for _, v in ipairs(type(p694) == "table" and p694 or { p694 }) do
            if table.find(v2108, v) and not table.find(names, v) then names[#names + 1] = v end
        end
        p693.config.names = names
        zeroinConfig.AutoMutateNames = table.clone(names)
        p693.manualConfirmation = nil p693.successHoldUntil = 0 p693.generation += 1
        p693:_emit() p693:_schedule()
        return table.clone(names)
    end
    t93.setMinValue = function(p695, p696)
        p695.config.minValuePerSec = math.max(tonumber(p696) or 0, 0)
        zeroinConfig.AutoMutateMinValue = p695.config.minValuePerSec
        p695.manualConfirmation = nil p695.successHoldUntil = 0 p695.generation += 1
        p695:_emit() p695:_schedule()
        return p695.config.minValuePerSec
    end
    t93.requestOnce = function(p697)
        if p697.running then return false, "Auto Consume Mutation is busy" end
        local v2114 = p697:_eligibleTargets()[1]
        if not v2114 then p697.manualConfirmation = nil p697:_setStatus(p697:_noTargetReason()) return false end
        local v2115 = table.concat({ p697.config.mutationType, v2114.uid }, ":")
        local mc = p697.manualConfirmation
        if mc and not (mc.signature ~= v2115 or mc.expiresAt < os.clock()) then
            p697.manualConfirmation = nil p697.generation += 1 p697:_schedule(true)
            return true
        end
        p697.manualConfirmation = { signature = v2115, expiresAt = os.clock() + 8 }
        p697:_setStatus(string.format("Ready: %s -> %s - press again", p697.config.mutationType, v2114.name))
        return false, "Press again within 8 seconds to confirm"
    end
    t93.getSnapshot = function(p698) return p698:_snapshot() end
    t93.destroy = function(p699)
        if p699.destroyed then return end
        p699.destroyed = true p699.config.enabled = false p699.generation += 1
        p699.coordinator:forceRelease("auto_mutate")
        for _, r in ipairs(p699.resources) do v2000(r) end
        table.clear(p699.resources) table.clear(p699.listeners)
    end
    return t93
end)()

local v2119Mutate = v1988Mutate.new(v593, v1327Runtime, nil, nil)

local v2120Capture = (function()
    local Players2 = Players
    local ReplicatedStorage2 = RS
    local LocalPlayer2 = lp
    local t104 = {}
    t104.__index = t104
    local vector32 = Vector3.new(8, 1, 8)
    t104.new = function()
        local obj = setmetatable({}, t104)
        obj.enabled = zeroinConfig.AutoCaptureEgg == true
        obj.destroyed = false obj.running = false obj.worker = nil
        obj.hoverPad = nil obj.hoverOrigin = nil obj.status = "Off"
        obj.changeCallbacks = {} obj.connections = {}
        obj:_bindSignals()
        if obj.enabled then obj:setEnabled(true) end
        return obj
    end
    t104.onChanged = function(p700, p701) if type(p701) == "function" then table.insert(p700.changeCallbacks, p701) end end
    t104._notify = function(p702, p703, p704)
        local t105 = { enabled = p702.enabled, status = p702.status, title = p703 or "Capture The Egg: " .. p702.status, content = p704 or "Waiting for event..." }
        for _, cb in ipairs(p702.changeCallbacks) do pcall(cb, t105) end
    end
    t104.isEventActive = function()
        return workspace:GetAttribute("Event_CaptureTheEgg") == true or workspace:GetAttribute("Event_CaptureTheEggGameplay") == true or workspace:GetAttribute("Event_CaptureTheEggUid") ~= nil
    end
    t104.getEventEggUid = function()
        local attr = workspace:GetAttribute("Event_CaptureTheEggUid")
        if attr and attr ~= "" then return tostring(attr) end
        local ok, result = pcall(function() return require(ReplicatedStorage2.Data.CaptureTheEgg) end)
        if ok and result and result.EggUidAttribute then
            local attr2 = workspace:GetAttribute(result.EggUidAttribute)
            if attr2 and attr2 ~= "" then return tostring(attr2) end
        end
        return nil
    end
    t104.isCarryingEventEgg = function(p705)
        local ok, result = pcall(function() return require(ReplicatedStorage2.Client.EggState) end)
        if not ok or not result then return false end
        local ReadLocalState = result.ReadLocalState and result.ReadLocalState()
        local v2137 = p705:getEventEggUid()
        if ReadLocalState and ReadLocalState.IsCarrying then
            if v2137 and ReadLocalState.Uid == v2137 then return true end
            if not v2137 then return true end
        end
        if v2137 then
            local v2138 = result.ReadFieldEgg(v2137)
            if v2138 and v2138.CarrierUserId == LocalPlayer2.UserId then return true end
        end
        return false
    end
    t104.isEngaged = function(p706) return not p706.destroyed and p706.enabled and p706:isEventActive() end
    t104._ensureHoverPad = function(p707, p708)
        if p707.hoverPad and p707.hoverPad.Parent then
            p707.hoverPad.CFrame = cFrameNew(p708.X, p708.Y - 3, p708.Z)
        else
            local Part = Instance.new("Part")
            Part.Name = "__CaptureHoverPad"
            Part.Size = vector32 Part.Anchored = true Part.CanCollide = true Part.Transparency = 1
            Part.CFrame = cFrameNew(p708.X, p708.Y - 3, p708.Z)
            Part.Parent = workspace
            p707.hoverPad = Part
        end
        return p707.hoverPad
    end
    t104._destroyHoverPad = function(p709) if p709.hoverPad then pcall(p709.hoverPad.Destroy, p709.hoverPad) p709.hoverPad = nil end end
    t104._startWorker = function(p710)
        if p710.worker then return end
        p710.running = true
        p710.worker = task.spawn(function()
            while not p710.destroyed and p710.enabled do
                if p710:isEventActive() then
                    local c = LocalPlayer2.Character
                    local hrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local Hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if hrp and Hum and not (Hum.Health <= 0) then
                        local v2143 = p710:getEventEggUid()
                        if p710:isCarryingEventEgg() then
                            if not p710.hoverOrigin then p710.hoverOrigin = Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X, math.max(hrp.Position.Y, 71) + 45, hrp.Position.Z) end
                            local Y = p710.hoverOrigin.Y
                            local v2145 = os.clock() * 0.35
                            local v33 = Vector3.new(p710.hoverOrigin.X + math.cos(v2145) * 12, Y, p710.hoverOrigin.Z + math.sin(v2145) * 12)
                            p710:_ensureHoverPad(v33)
                            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                            hrp.CFrame = cFrameNew(v33)
                            p710.status = "Holding Egg (+45 studs)"
                            p710:_notify("Holding Event Egg", "Hovering at safe altitude (+45 studs)")
                            task.wait(0.05)
                        else
                            p710:_destroyHoverPad()
                            p710.hoverOrigin = nil
                            local ok, result = pcall(function() return require(ReplicatedStorage2.Client.EggState) end)
                            local v2149 = ok and v2143 and result.ReadFieldEgg(v2143)
                            if v2149 and v2149.CarrierUserId and v2149.CarrierUserId ~= LocalPlayer2.UserId then
                                local pb = Players2:GetPlayerByUserId(v2149.CarrierUserId)
                                local c2 = pb and pb.Character
                                local hrp2 = c2 and c2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                if hrp2 then
                                    p710.status = "Attacking Carrier"
                                    p710:_notify("Attacking Carrier", "Chasing " .. tostring(pb.DisplayName or pb.Name))
                                    local v2153 = hrp2.Position - hrp.Position
                                    local Magnitude = v2153.Magnitude
                                    if Magnitude > 7 then
                                        hrp.CFrame = cFrameNew(hrp.Position + v2153.Unit * math.min(Magnitude - 4, 4.25), hrp2.Position)
                                    else
                                        hrp.CFrame = cFrameNew(hrp2.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0))
                                        if genv.OutlawGodMode then
                                            local v2155 = genv.OutlawGodMode:_equipBat()
                                            if v2155 then genv.OutlawGodMode:_swing(v2155, hrp2) end
                                        end
                                    end
                                else p710.status = "Tracking Carrier" p710:_notify("Tracking Carrier", "Carrier is moving or respawning") end
                                task.wait(0.05)
                            else
                                p710.status = "Securing Egg"
                                p710:_notify("Securing Event Egg", "Flying to egg to snatch it")
                                local v2156
                                if v2149 and v2149.BottomCFrame then v2156 = v2149.BottomCFrame.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
                                else
                                    local sp = workspace:FindFirstChild("CaptureTheEggSpawn", true) or workspace:FindFirstChild("AdminAbuseEggSpawn", true)
                                    if sp and sp:IsA("BasePart") then v2156 = sp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0) end
                                end
                                if v2156 then
                                    local v2158 = v2156 - hrp.Position
                                    local Magnitude = v2158.Magnitude
                                    if Magnitude > 6 then hrp.CFrame = cFrameNew(hrp.Position + v2158.Unit * math.min(Magnitude, 4.5))
                                    else
                                        hrp.CFrame = cFrameNew(v2156)
                                        local Rem = require(ReplicatedStorage2.Shared.Remotes)
                                        if Rem and Rem.EggWorld and Rem.EggWorld.AskFieldEggCarry then
                                            pcall(function() Rem.EggWorld.AskFieldEggCarry:InvokeServer({ Uid = v2143 }) end)
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                                task.wait(0.05)
                            end
                        end
                    else p710:_destroyHoverPad() task.wait(0.5) end
                else
                    p710:_destroyHoverPad()
                    p710.hoverOrigin = nil
                    p710.status = "Waiting for event"
                    p710:_notify("Capture The Egg Event", "Waiting for event to begin...")
                    task.wait(1)
                end
            end
            p710:_destroyHoverPad()
            p710.running = false
            p710.worker = nil
        end)
    end
    t104.setEnabled = function(p711, p712)
        local enabled = p712 == true
        if p711.enabled == enabled and p711.worker then return end
        p711.enabled = enabled
        zeroinConfig.AutoCaptureEgg = enabled
        if enabled then p711:_startWorker()
        else
            if p711.worker then pcall(task.cancel, p711.worker) p711.worker = nil end
            p711:_destroyHoverPad()
            p711.hoverOrigin = nil p711.running = false p711.status = "Off"
            p711:_notify("Capture The Egg Event", "Auto Capture is disabled")
        end
    end
    t104._bindSignals = function(p713)
        pcall(function()
            table.insert(p713.connections, workspace:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Event_CaptureTheEgg"):Connect(function() if p713.enabled and not p713.destroyed then p713:_startWorker() end end))
            table.insert(p713.connections, workspace:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Event_CaptureTheEggGameplay"):Connect(function() if p713.enabled and not p713.destroyed then p713:_startWorker() end end))
            table.insert(p713.connections, workspace:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Event_CaptureTheEggUid"):Connect(function() if p713.enabled and not p713.destroyed then p713:_startWorker() end end))
        end)
    end
    t104.destroy = function(p714)
        if p714.destroyed then return end
        p714.destroyed = true p714:setEnabled(false)
        for _, c in ipairs(p714.connections) do pcall(c.Disconnect, c) end
        table.clear(p714.connections)
        p714:_destroyHoverPad()
    end
    return t104
end)()

local v2164Capture = v2120Capture.new()

local v2165Treadmill = (function()
    local Players2 = Players
    local ReplicatedStorage2 = RS
    local LocalPlayer2 = lp
    local Remotes = Remotes
    local PlotState2 = PlotState
    local t106 = {}
    t106.__index = t106
    t106.new = function(p715, p716)
        local obj = setmetatable({}, t106)
        obj.coordinator = p715 obj.travel = p716 obj.destroyed = false
        obj.listeners = {} obj.resources = {}
        obj.config = { disableDuringAutomation = true, idleFarm = false }
        obj.stats = { mode = "Idle", boost = "Waiting" }
        obj.busy = p715 and p715:getSnapshot().busy == true or false
        obj.idleGeneration = 0 obj.lastEquipAt = 0 obj.activeTreadmill = nil
        obj.travelingToTreadmill = false obj.treadmillEquipPending = false
        obj.unequipGuardRunning = false obj.backgroundYieldRunning = false
        obj.lastBackgroundYieldAt = 0 obj.treadmillStateRevision = 0
        obj:_listenActiveTreadmill() obj:_syncActiveTreadmillSnapshot()
        obj:_listenCoordinator() obj:_listenSpeedGain()
        return obj
    end
    t106._snapshot = function(p717)
        return { mode = p717.stats.mode, boost = p717.stats.boost, disableDuringAutomation = p717.config.disableDuringAutomation, idleFarm = p717.config.idleFarm }
    end
    t106._emit = function(p718)
        local v2173 = p718:_snapshot()
        for _, l in ipairs(p718.listeners) do pcall(l, v2173) end
    end
    t106._setStatus = function(p719, p720, p721)
        if p720 ~= nil then p719.stats.mode = p720 end
        if p721 ~= nil then p719.stats.boost = p721 end
        p719:_emit()
    end
    t106.subscribe = function(p722, p723)
        table.insert(p722.listeners, p723)
        pcall(p723, p722:_snapshot())
        return function()
            local i = table.find(p722.listeners, p723)
            if i then table.remove(p722.listeners, i) end
        end
    end
    t106._requestUnequip = function()
        local ok, result, result2 = pcall(function() return Remotes.Treadmill.AskDoff:InvokeServer() end)
        if not ok then return false, tostring(result) end
        if result == false then return false, tostring(result2 or "Server rejected") end
        return true
    end
    t106._shouldBlockTreadmill = function(p724)
        local v2182 = not p724.destroyed
        return v2182 and p724.busy and p724.config.disableDuringAutomation
    end
    t106._startUnequipGuard = function(p725)
        if p725.unequipGuardRunning or not p725:_shouldBlockTreadmill() or p725.activeTreadmill == nil then return end
        p725.unequipGuardRunning = true
        task.spawn(function()
            local n33 = 0.15
            while p725:_shouldBlockTreadmill() do
                if p725.activeTreadmill == nil then break end
                local v2184, v2185 = p725:_requestUnequip()
                p725:_setStatus("Automation active", v2184 and "Blocked" or "Release retry: " .. tostring(v2185))
                task.wait(n33)
                n33 = math.min(n33 * 2, 0.75)
            end
            p725.unequipGuardRunning = false
            if p725:_shouldBlockTreadmill() and p725.activeTreadmill ~= nil then p725:_startUnequipGuard() end
        end)
    end
    t106._cancelIdle = function(p726, p727)
        p726.idleGeneration += 1
        local gen = p726.idleGeneration
        if p727 then p726:_requestUnequip() end
        task.delay(0.5, function()
            if not p726.destroyed and not p726.busy then
                if p726.config.idleFarm and p726.idleGeneration == gen then p726:_startIdle() end
            end
        end)
    end
    t106.setDisableDuringAutomation = function(p728, p729)
        p728.config.disableDuringAutomation = p729 == true
        if p728.busy and p728.config.disableDuringAutomation then
            p728:_cancelIdle(true) p728:_startUnequipGuard()
            p728:_setStatus("Automation active", "Blocked")
        elseif not p728.busy and p728.config.idleFarm then p728:_startIdle() end
    end
    t106.setIdleFarm = function(p730, p731)
        p730.config.idleFarm = p731 == true
        if not p730.config.idleFarm then
            p730:_cancelIdle(true)
            p730:_setStatus(p730.busy and "Automation active" or "Idle", "Disabled")
        elseif not p730.busy then p730:_startIdle() end
    end
    t106.setCaptureTheEggRuntime = function(p732, p733) p732.captureTheEggRuntime = p733 end
    t106._resolveStandCFrame = function()
        local slot = PlotState2.ResolveLocalSlot()
        local folder = PlotState2.ResolveFolder()
        local plot = folder and slot and folder:FindFirstChild(tostring(slot))
        local TB = plot and plot:FindFirstChild("TreadmillBottom")
        if not TB or not TB:IsA("BasePart") then return nil end
        local c = LocalPlayer2.Character
        local hrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local Hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hrp or not Hum then return nil end
        local v2194
        if Hum.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 then v2194 = hrp.Size.Y * 0.5 + math.max(Hum.HipHeight, 0.5)
        else v2194 = hrp.Size.Y * 0.5 + 2 end
        return TB.CFrame * cFrameNew(0, TB.Size.Y * 0.5 + v2194 + 0.05, 0)
    end
    t106.isEngaged = function(p734) return p734.activeTreadmill ~= nil or p734.travelingToTreadmill or p734.treadmillEquipPending end
    t106.yieldForBackground = function(p735)
        if p735.destroyed then return end
        p735:_cancelIdle(false)
        if p735.backgroundYieldRunning then return end
        p735.backgroundYieldRunning = true
        task.spawn(function()
            local n34 = 0.15
            while not p735.destroyed do
                if p735.coordinator and p735.coordinator:isBackgroundBusy() then
                    p735.lastBackgroundYieldAt = os.clock()
                    p735:_setStatus("Idle", "Yielding to background inventory")
                    p735:_requestUnequip()
                    task.wait(n34)
                    if p735:_syncActiveTreadmillSnapshot() then n34 = math.min(n34 * 2, 0.75) continue end
                end
                break
            end
            p735.backgroundYieldRunning = false
            if not p735.destroyed and not p735:isEngaged() then p735:_setStatus("Idle", "Background inventory ready") end
        end)
    end
    t106._startIdle = function(p736)
        if p736.destroyed or p736.busy or not p736.config.idleFarm then return end
        p736.idleGeneration += 1
        local gen = p736.idleGeneration
        local now = os.clock()
        task.spawn(function()
            local ok, result = xpcall(function()
                while not p736.destroyed and not p736.busy and p736.config.idleFarm and gen == p736.idleGeneration do
                    if p736.captureTheEggRuntime and type(p736.captureTheEggRuntime.isEngaged) == "function" and p736.captureTheEggRuntime:isEngaged() then
                        now = os.clock() p736:_setStatus("Idle", "Yielding to Capture The Egg") task.wait(0.25)
                        continue
                    end
                    if p736.coordinator and p736.coordinator:isBackgroundBusy() then
                        now = os.clock() p736:_setStatus("Idle", "Waiting for background inventory") task.wait(0.25)
                        continue
                    end
                    if p736.eggRuntime and p736.eggRuntime.enabled and not p736.eggRuntime.resetBlocked then
                        local v2200 = false
                        if p736.eggRuntime.records then
                            for _, rec in pairs(p736.eggRuntime.records) do if p736.eggRuntime:_matches(rec) then v2200 = true break end end
                        end
                        if v2200 then now = os.clock() p736:_setStatus("Idle", "Yielding to Auto Steal") task.wait(0.25) continue end
                    end
                    if genv.OutlawIsAutoSellEggBusy and genv.OutlawIsAutoSellEggBusy() then
                        now = os.clock() p736:_setStatus("Idle", "Waiting for Auto Sell Egg") task.wait(0.25)
                    else
                        local v2203 = 1 - (os.clock() - now)
                        if v2203 > 0 then p736:_setStatus("Idle", string.format("Starting in %.0fs", math.ceil(v2203))) task.wait(math.min(v2203, 0.25))
                        else
                            local v2204 = p736:_resolveStandCFrame()
                            if v2204 then
                                p736.travelingToTreadmill = true
                                p736:_setStatus("Moving", "Waiting")
                                local travel = p736.travel and p736.travel:travelAndWait(v2204, {
                                    abort = function()
                                        local d = p736.destroyed or p736.busy
                                        return d or not p736.config.idleFarm or gen ~= p736.idleGeneration or p736.coordinator and p736.coordinator:isBackgroundBusy() or genv.OutlawIsAutoSellEggBusy and genv.OutlawIsAutoSellEggBusy()
                                    end
                                })
                                p736.travelingToTreadmill = false
                                if not travel then
                                    if not p736.busy and p736.config.idleFarm then p736:_setStatus("Idle", "Travel failed") task.wait(1) end
                                elseif not p736.busy and p736.config.idleFarm and gen == p736.idleGeneration then
                                    if not (p736.coordinator and p736.coordinator:isBackgroundBusy()) then
                                        if not genv.OutlawIsAutoSellEggBusy or not genv.OutlawIsAutoSellEggBusy() then
                                            task.wait(0.35)
                                            if not p736.busy and p736.config.idleFarm and gen == p736.idleGeneration then
                                                if not (p736.coordinator and p736.coordinator:isBackgroundBusy()) then
                                                    if not genv.OutlawIsAutoSellEggBusy or not genv.OutlawIsAutoSellEggBusy() then
                                                        p736.treadmillEquipPending = true
                                                        local ok3, result, result2 = pcall(function() return Remotes.Treadmill.AskWearStill:InvokeServer() end)
                                                        if ok3 and result ~= false then
                                                            p736.lastEquipAt = os.clock()
                                                            p736:_setStatus("Active", "Waiting for gain")
                                                            task.delay(2, function() if not p736.destroyed and p736.activeTreadmill == nil then p736.treadmillEquipPending = false end end)
                                                        else
                                                            p736.treadmillEquipPending = false
                                                            p736:_setStatus("Preparing carry", "Activation failed: " .. tostring(result2 or result))
                                                        end
                                                        local v2210 = os.clock() + 8
                                                        while (p736.activeTreadmill ~= nil or os.clock() < v2210) and not p736.destroyed and not p736.busy and p736.config.idleFarm and gen == p736.idleGeneration do
                                                            if p736.coordinator and p736.coordinator:isBackgroundBusy() then break end
                                                            if p736.captureTheEggRuntime and type(p736.captureTheEggRuntime.isEngaged) == "function" and p736.captureTheEggRuntime:isEngaged() then
                                                                p736:_cancelIdle(true) p736:_setStatus("Idle", "Yielding to Capture The Egg")
                                                                break
                                                            end
                                                            if not p736.eggRuntime or not p736.eggRuntime.enabled or p736.eggRuntime.resetBlocked then
                                                                if genv.OutlawIsAutoSellEggBusy and genv.OutlawIsAutoSellEggBusy() then
                                                                    p736:_cancelIdle(true) p736:_setStatus("Idle", "Yielding to Auto Sell Egg")
                                                                    break
                                                                end
                                                                task.wait(0.25)
                                                                continue
                                                            end
                                                            local v2213 = false
                                                            if p736.eggRuntime.records then
                                                                for _, rec in pairs(p736.eggRuntime.records) do if p736.eggRuntime:_matches(rec) then v2213 = true break end end
                                                            end
                                                            if v2213 then p736:_cancelIdle(true) p736:_setStatus("Idle", "Yielding to Auto Steal") break end
                                                            if genv.OutlawIsAutoSellEggBusy and genv.OutlawIsAutoSellEggBusy() then
                                                                p736:_cancelIdle(true) p736:_setStatus("Idle", "Yielding to Auto Sell Egg")
                                                                break
                                                            end
                                                            task.wait(0.25)
                                                        end
                                                    end
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            else p736:_setStatus("Idle", "Position unavailable") task.wait(2) end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end, debug.traceback)
            if not ok then warn("[Outlaw][treadmill] idle error:\n" .. tostring(result)) end
        end)
    end
    t106._onCoordinator = function(p737, p738)
        local busy = p737.busy
        p737.busy = p738.busy == true
        if p737.busy then
            p737:_cancelIdle(p737.config.disableDuringAutomation)
            p737:_startUnequipGuard()
            p737:_setStatus("Automation active", p737.config.disableDuringAutomation and "Blocked" or "Unchanged")
        elseif busy then
            if p737.config.idleFarm then p737:_startIdle()
            else p737:_setStatus("Idle", "Disabled") end
        end
    end
    t106._listenCoordinator = function(p739)
        if not p739.coordinator then return end
        table.insert(p739.resources, p739.coordinator:subscribe(function(p740) p739:_onCoordinator(p740) end))
        table.insert(p739.resources, p739.coordinator:subscribeBackground(function(p741)
            if p741.busy then
                p739:_cancelIdle(false)
                p739:_setStatus("Idle", "Waiting for background inventory")
            elseif not p739.destroyed and not p739.busy and p739.config.idleFarm then p739:_startIdle() end
        end))
    end
    t106._syncActiveTreadmillSnapshot = function(p742)
        local ok, result, rev = pcall(function() return Remotes.Treadmill.AskRenderSnapshot:InvokeServer() end)
        if not ok or type(result) ~= "table" then return p742.activeTreadmill ~= nil end
        if type(rev) == "number" and rev < p742.treadmillStateRevision then return p742.activeTreadmill ~= nil end
        local v2220 = table.find(result, LocalPlayer2.UserId) ~= nil
        if type(rev) == "number" then p742.treadmillStateRevision = rev end
        p742.activeTreadmill = v2220 and "snapshot" or nil
        if not v2220 then p742.treadmillEquipPending = false end
        return v2220
    end
    t106._listenActiveTreadmill = function(p743)
        local R = Remotes.Treadmill.RenderStateShifted
        if R then
            table.insert(p743.resources, R.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(p744, p745, p746)
                if p744 ~= LocalPlayer2 or type(p745) ~= "boolean" then return end
                if type(p746) == "number" and p746 <= p743.treadmillStateRevision then return end
                p743.treadmillStateRevision = type(p746) == "number" and p746 or p743.treadmillStateRevision + 1
                p743.activeTreadmill = p745 and true or nil
                p743.treadmillEquipPending = false
                if p745 and p743:_shouldBlockTreadmill() then p743:_startUnequipGuard()
                elseif not p745 and p743:_shouldBlockTreadmill() then p743:_setStatus("Automation active", "Blocked")
                elseif not p745 then p743:_setStatus("Idle", p743.config.idleFarm and "Released" or "Disabled")
                elseif not p743.busy then p743:_setStatus("Active", "Waiting for gain") end
            end))
        end
    end
    t106._listenSpeedGain = function(p747)
        local G = Remotes.Treadmill.SpeedGained
        if G then
            table.insert(p747.resources, G.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(p748, p749)
                if p749 ~= "Treadmill" then return end
                p747.activeTreadmill = p747.activeTreadmill or true
                p747.treadmillEquipPending = false
                if p747:_shouldBlockTreadmill() then p747:_startUnequipGuard() return end
                p747:_setStatus("Active", "Gain ticking")
            end))
        end
    end
    t106.destroy = function(p750)
        if p750.destroyed then return end
        p750.destroyed = true p750:_cancelIdle(true)
        for _, r in ipairs(p750.resources) do
            pcall(function()
                if typeof(r) == "RBXScriptConnection" then r:Disconnect()
                elseif type(r) == "function" then r()
                elseif type(r) == "table" and type(r.Disconnect) == "function" then r:Disconnect() end
            end)
        end
        table.clear(p750.resources) table.clear(p750.listeners)
    end
    t106.Destroy = t106.destroy
    return t106
end)()

t20 = {}

t20.AutoPlaceLoop = function()
    if zeroinConfig.IsAutoPlaceWorkerRunning then return end
    zeroinConfig.IsAutoPlaceWorkerRunning = true
    while zeroinConfig.AutoPlace or zeroinConfig.AutoRift do
        local autoPlace
        local ok, result = pcall(function()
            if zeroinConfig.IsStealing then return end
            local RS2 = RS
            local EggState2 = require(RS2.Client.EggState)
            local Assets = require(RS2.Data.Assets)
            local PlotState2 = require(RS2.Client.PlotState)
            local slot = PlotState2.ResolveLocalSlot and PlotState2.ResolveLocalSlot() or PlotState2.GetMySlot()
            local folder = PlotState2.ResolveFolder and PlotState2.ResolveFolder() or PlotState2.GetPlotsFolder() or workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
            local plot = folder and slot and folder:FindFirstChild(tostring(slot))
            if not plot and folder then
                for _, child in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                    local ps = child:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
                    local pps = ps and ps:FindFirstChild("PlayerPlotSign")
                    if pps and pps:FindFirstChild("Frame") and pps.Frame:FindFirstChild("PlayerName") then
                        local n = string.gsub(pps.Frame.PlayerName.Text, "<[^>]+>", "")
                        if string.find(string.lower(n), string.lower(lp.Name)) or string.find(string.lower(n), string.lower(lp.DisplayName)) then plot = child break end
                    end
                end
            end
            if not plot then return end
            local TU = plot:FindFirstChild("ToUpdate")
            local PA = TU and TU:FindFirstChild("PetArea")
            local CP = plot:FindFirstChild("CenterPoint")
            CP = CP or TU and TU:FindFirstChild("CenterPoint") or PA
            if not PA or not PA:IsA("BasePart") then return end
            local function snap()
                local t126, t127 = {}, {}
                local Save = require(RS2.Shared.Save)
                pcall(function()
                    local v2402 = EggState2.GetOwnerRuntimeRecords(lp.UserId)
                    if type(v2402) == "table" then for k, v in pairs(v2402) do t127[k] = v end end
                end)
                pcall(function()
                    local v2405 = Save.Get()
                    local EI = v2405 and v2405.EggInventory
                    if type(EI) == "table" then for k, v in pairs(EI) do if not t127[k] then t127[k] = v end end end
                end)
                local v2409 = table.clone(zeroinConfig.RiftObjectiveCategories)
                local n35 = 0
                local t128 = {}
                for k, v in pairs(t127) do
                    if v.Placement == nil then
                        local AC = v.AssetCategory
                        local v2415 = AC and Assets.Directory[AC]
                        local s5 = "Unknown"
                        if v2415 and v2415.Rarity then
                            local R = v2415.Rarity
                            s5 = R.DisplayName or R._id or R.Name or "Unknown"
                        end
                        local v2418
                        if #zeroinConfig.PlaceRarities == 0 or table.find(zeroinConfig.PlaceRarities, "None") or table.find(zeroinConfig.PlaceRarities, "All") then v2418 = true
                        else v2418 = table.find(zeroinConfig.PlaceRarities, s5) ~= nil end
                        local v2419 = tonumber(v.Kg) or tonumber(v.Weight) or tonumber(v.kg)
                        if not v2419 and v2415 then v2419 = tonumber(v2415.Kg) or tonumber(v2415.Weight) or tonumber(v2415.kg) end
                        local v2420 = v2419 or 0
                        local v2421 = (v2409[AC] or 0) > 0
                        if v2421 or zeroinConfig.AutoPlace and v2418 then
                            table.insert(t126, { Uid = k, Rarity = s5, Kg = v2420, IsRift = v2421 })
                            if v2421 then v2409[AC] -= 1 end
                        end
                    else
                        n35 += 1
                        local P
                        if typeof(v.Placement) == "CFrame" then P = v.Placement.Position
                        elseif typeof(v.Placement) == "Vector3" then P = v.Placement
                        elseif type(v.Placement) == "table" and v.Placement.Position then P = v.Placement.Position end
                        if P then table.insert(t128, P) end
                    end
                end
                table.sort(t126, function(a, b)
                    if a.IsRift == b.IsRift then return a.Kg > b.Kg end
                    return a.IsRift == true
                end)
                return t126, n35, t128
            end
            local v2423, v2424 = snap()
            local c = lp.Character
            local hrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local Hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local CF2
            if #v2423 == 0 or v2424 >= 30 then return end
            autoPlace = v593:tryAcquire("auto_place", {})
            if not autoPlace then return end
            if hrp then CF2 = hrp.CFrame end
            if zeroinConfig.IsStealing then return end
            zeroinConfig.IsPlacing = true
            local function abort() return not zeroinConfig.AutoPlace and not zeroinConfig.AutoRift or zeroinConfig.IsStealing or zeroinConfig.IsRiftTrading or zeroinConfig.IsScrambleFarming end
            if hrp then
                local v2431 = PA.CFrame * cFrameNew(0, 3, 0)
                local function isClear(a, b, inst)
                    local u = true
                    pcall(function()
                        local filter = { inst }
                        local rp = RaycastParams.new()
                        rp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
                        local cur = a
                        for i = 1, 8 do
                            rp.FilterDescendantsInstances = filter
                            local d = b - cur
                            if not (d.Magnitude < 1) then
                                local hit = workspace:Raycast(cur, d, rp)
                                if hit then
                                    if hit.Instance.CanCollide then u = false break end
                                    table.insert(filter, hit.Instance)
                                    cur = hit.Position + d.Unit * 0.1
                                    continue
                                end
                            end
                            break
                        end
                    end)
                    return u
                end
                if not isClear(hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0), v2431.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0), c) then
                    v339(hrp, Hum, cFrameNew(518.66, 70.57, -364.73) * Angles(math.pi, -1.5676547341413067, math.pi), abort)
                    if abort() then return end
                end
                v339(hrp, Hum, v2431, abort)
                if abort() then return end
                local n36 = 0
                while n36 < 0.3 and not abort() do task.wait(0.05) n36 += 0.05 end
                if zeroinConfig.IsStealing then return end
                if not zeroinConfig.AutoPlace and not zeroinConfig.AutoRift then return end
            end
            local n37 = 0
            local v2434 = false
            while not v2434 and (zeroinConfig.AutoPlace or zeroinConfig.AutoRift) and not abort() do
                local v2435, v2436, v2437 = snap()
                if #v2435 ~= 0 and not (v2436 >= 30) then
                    local v2438 = v2436 local v2439 = v2437
                    local v2440 = false
                    for _, v in ipairs(v2435) do
                        if not zeroinConfig.AutoPlace and not zeroinConfig.AutoRift or v2434 or abort() then break end
                        if v2438 >= 30 then v2434 = true break end
                        local Uid = v.Uid
                        if EggState2.RequestEquipTool(Uid) and not abort() then
                            task.wait(0.15)
                            local v2444 = false
                            while n37 < 30 and not v2444 and not abort() do
                                local v2445
                                if CP then
                                    while n37 < 30 do
                                        local v2446 = PA.Size.X - 6
                                        local v2447 = v2446 / 10
                                        local v2448 = CP.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(PA.CFrame * Vector3.new(-(v2446 / 2) + v2447 / 2 + n37 % 10 * v2447, 0, -((PA.Size.Z - 6) / 2) + 3 + math.floor(n37 / 10) * 6))
                                        local cF = cFrameNew(v2448.X, -0.5, v2448.Z)
                                        local v2450 = false
                                        for _, v2 in ipairs(v2439) do if (v2 - cF.Position).Magnitude < 1 then v2450 = true break end end
                                        if not v2450 then v2445 = cF break end
                                        n37 += 1
                                    end
                                end
                                if not v2445 then v2434 = true break end
                                if not abort() then
                                    local v2453, v2454 = EggState2.RequestPlaceEgg(Uid, v2445)
                                    if v2453 then
                                        table.insert(v2439, v2445.Position)
                                        v2438 += 1 v2444 = true v2440 = true n37 += 1
                                        task.wait(0.15)
                                        continue
                                    end
                                    local v2455 = v2454 and string.lower(tostring(v2454)) or ""
                                    if not string.find(v2455, "full") and not string.find(v2455, "limit") and not string.find(v2455, "capacity") and not string.find(v2455, "maximum") then
                                        table.insert(v2439, v2445.Position) n37 += 1
                                        task.wait(0.1)
                                        continue
                                    end
                                    if zeroinConfig.AutoPlace then zeroinConfig.AutoPlace = false end
                                    v2434 = true
                                    break
                                end
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if v2440 then task.wait(0.2) continue end
                end
                break
            end
            if CF2 and hrp and not zeroinConfig.IsStealing then
                local function isClear(a, b, inst)
                    local u = true
                    pcall(function()
                        local filter = { inst }
                        local rp = RaycastParams.new()
                        rp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
                        local cur = a
                        for i = 1, 8 do
                            rp.FilterDescendantsInstances = filter
                            local d = b - cur
                            if not (d.Magnitude < 1) then
                                local hit = workspace:Raycast(cur, d, rp)
                                if hit then
                                    if hit.Instance.CanCollide then u = false break end
                                    table.insert(filter, hit.Instance)
                                    cur = hit.Position + d.Unit * 0.1
                                    continue
                                end
                            end
                            break
                        end
                    end)
                    return u
                end
                if not isClear(hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0), CF2.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0), c) then
                    v339(hrp, Hum, cFrameNew(518.66, 70.57, -364.73) * Angles(math.pi, -1.5676547341413067, math.pi), abort)
                end
                if abort() then return end
                v339(hrp, Hum, CF2, abort)
                task.wait(0.2)
            end
        end)
        if autoPlace then v593:release(autoPlace) autoPlace = nil end
        zeroinConfig.IsPlacing = false
        if not ok then warn("Auto Place Error: " .. tostring(result)) end
        task.wait(1.5)
    end
    zeroinConfig.IsAutoPlaceWorkerRunning = false
end

t20.AutoHatchLoop = function()
    if zeroinConfig.IsAutoHatchWorkerRunning then return end
    zeroinConfig.IsAutoHatchWorkerRunning = true
    task.spawn(function()
        local t129 = {}
        while zeroinConfig.AutoHatch or zeroinConfig.AutoRift do
            task.wait(1.5)
            pcall(function()
                local EggState2 = require(RS.Client.EggState)
                local v2459 = EggState2.GetOwnerRuntimeRecords(lp.UserId)
                if not v2459 then return end
                local t130 = {}
                local v2461 = table.clone(zeroinConfig.RiftObjectiveCategories)
                for k, v in pairs(v2459) do
                    if v.Placement ~= nil then
                        if not zeroinConfig.AutoHatch then
                            if not ((v2461[v.AssetCategory] or 0) > 0) then continue end
                        end
                        table.insert(t130, k)
                        if not zeroinConfig.AutoHatch then v2461[v.AssetCategory] = (v2461[v.AssetCategory] or 1) - 1 end
                    end
                end
                local now = os.time()
                for _, v in ipairs(t130) do
                    if not zeroinConfig.AutoHatch and not zeroinConfig.AutoRift then break end
                    if not t129[v] or not (now < t129[v]) then
                        local v2467 = v2459[v]
                        if zeroinConfig.AutoHatch or v2467 and type(zeroinConfig.RiftObjectiveCategories) == "table" and (zeroinConfig.RiftObjectiveCategories[v2467.AssetCategory] or 0) > 0 then
                            local v2468 = false
                            if type(EggState2.IsReadyToHatch) == "function" then
                                local ok, result = pcall(EggState2.IsReadyToHatch, v)
                                v2468 = ok and result == true
                            end
                            if v2468 or v2467.Ready == true or not t129[v] then
                                if EggState2.RequestHatchEgg(v) then
                                    EggState2.RequestCompleteHatchEgg(v)
                                    t129[v] = nil
                                    task.wait(0.1)
                                else t129[v] = now + 10 end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
        zeroinConfig.IsAutoHatchWorkerRunning = false
    end)
end

t20.startAutoSellLoop = function()
    if t20.__sellLoopRunning then return end
    t20.__sellLoopRunning = true
    task.spawn(function()
        local RS2 = RS
        local Assets = require(RS2.Data.Assets)
        local Save = require(RS2.Shared.Save)
        local AssetRoster = require(RS2.Client.AssetRoster)
        local Remotes = require(RS2.Shared.Remotes)
        local lp2 = lp
        local function placedUids()
            local ok, result = pcall(function() return AssetRoster.ReadOwnerPen(lp2.UserId) end)
            if not ok or type(result) ~= "table" then return nil end
            local t136 = {}
            for k, v in pairs(result) do
                if type(k) == "string" then t136[k] = true end
                if type(v) == "table" and v.UID then t136[v.UID] = true end
            end
            return t136
        end
        while zeroinConfig.AutoSell do
            local ok, result = pcall(function()
                local save = Save.Get()
                local Inventory = save and save.Inventory or {}
                local placed = placedUids()
                if not placed then return end
                for k, v in pairs(Inventory) do
                    if type(v) == "table" then
                        local isPlaced = placed[k] == true
                        local isFav = v.IsFavorite == true or v["Jadikan Favorit"] == true or v.Favorite == true
                        local isEq = table.find(save.EquippedAssets or {}, k) ~= nil
                        local isInFuse = v.InFuse == true
                        local cat = v.Category or v.id or v.AssetCategory or v.ItemId
                        local dir = cat and Assets.Directory[cat]
                        local dn = dir and (dir.DisplayName or dir._id or dir.Name)
                        dn = dn or "Unknown"
                        local rar = dir and dir.Rarity
                        local rn = rar and (rar.DisplayName or rar._id or rar.Name) or "Unknown"
                        if v325(dn, rn, zeroinConfig.SellNames, zeroinConfig.SellRarities) and not isFav and not isPlaced and not isEq and not isInFuse then
                            if not v593:isCategoryProtected(cat, "auto_sell_pets") then
                                local bg = v593:tryAcquireBackground("auto_sell_pets", { k })
                                if bg then
                                    local Character = lp2.Character
                                    local Hum = Character and Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                    local tool
                                    for _, holder in ipairs({ lp2.Backpack, Character }) do
                                        if holder then
                                            for _, ch in ipairs(holder:GetChildren()) do
                                                if ch:IsA("Tool") and ch:GetAttribute("UID") == k then tool = ch break end
                                            end
                                        end
                                        if tool then break end
                                    end
                                    if tool and Hum then
                                        Hum:EquipTool(tool)
                                        task.wait(0.2)
                                        Remotes.PetSatchel.SellPet:FireServer({ k })
                                        task.wait(0.05)
                                    end
                                    v593:release(bg)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            if not ok then warn("Auto Sell Error:", result) end
            task.wait(1.5)
        end
        t20.__sellLoopRunning = false
    end)
end

t20.startAutoSellEggLoop = function()
    if t20.__sellEggRunning then return end
    t20.__sellEggRunning = true
    task.spawn(function()
        local RS2 = RS
        local Directory = require(RS2.Data.Assets).Directory
        local EggState2 = require(RS2.Client.EggState)
        local Remotes = require(RS2.Shared.Remotes)
        local Save = require(RS2.Shared.Save)
        while zeroinConfig.AutoSellEgg do
            local v2630 = zeroinConfig.SellEggRarities
            local v2631 = Save.Get()
            local EI = v2631 and v2631.EggInventory
            if v2630 and type(EI) == "table" then
                for k, v in pairs(EI) do
                    if zeroinConfig.AutoSellEgg then
                        local dir = type(v) == "table" and Directory[v.AssetCategory]
                        local dn = dir and dir.Rarity and (dir.Rarity.DisplayName or dir.Rarity._id)
                        if v.Placement == nil and not v266(v.AssetCategory) and not v593:isCategoryProtected(v.AssetCategory, "auto_sell") then
                            if v328(dn or "Unknown", v2630) then
                                local bg = v593:tryAcquireBackground("auto_sell", { k })
                                if bg then
                                    if EggState2.WearEggTool(k) then
                                        task.wait(0.2)
                                        Remotes.PetSatchel.SellPet:FireServer({ k })
                                        task.wait(0.25)
                                    end
                                    v593:release(bg)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.5)
        end
        t20.__sellEggRunning = false
    end)
end

local function v632RuntimeCleanup()
    if v2237Lifecycle then v2237Lifecycle:destroy() end
    if v1586Rift then v1586Rift:destroy() end
    v593:forceRelease("auto_the_rift")
    v593:forceRelease("auto_place")
    if genv.OutlawRiftRuntime == v1586Rift then genv.OutlawRiftRuntime = nil end
    if genv.OutlawSetAutoRift == v2260 then genv.OutlawSetAutoRift = nil end
    if v1327Runtime then pcall(v1327Runtime.destroy, v1327Runtime) end
    if v2225Treadmill then pcall(v2225Treadmill.destroy, v2225Treadmill) end
    if v2164Capture then pcall(v2164Capture.destroy, v2164Capture) end
    if genv.OutlawEggRuntime == v1327Runtime then genv.OutlawEggRuntime = nil end
    if genv.OutlawSetStealFromPlayers == v2264 then genv.OutlawSetStealFromPlayers = nil end
    if genv.OutlawSetDropAtFirstArea == v2267 then genv.OutlawSetDropAtFirstArea = nil end
    if genv.OutlawTreadmillManager == v2225Treadmill then genv.OutlawTreadmillManager = nil end
    if genv.OutlawCaptureTheEggRuntime == v2164Capture then genv.OutlawCaptureTheEggRuntime = nil end
    if v1377Runtime then pcall(v1377Runtime.destroy, v1377Runtime) end
    if v1437GodMode then pcall(v1437GodMode.destroy, v1437GodMode) end
    if genv.OutlawAntiGuard == v1377Runtime then genv.OutlawAntiGuard = nil end
    if genv.OutlawGodMode == v1437GodMode then genv.OutlawGodMode = nil end
    if v1775Scramble then pcall(v1775Scramble.destroy, v1775Scramble) end
    if genv.OutlawScrambleRuntime == v1775Scramble then
        genv.OutlawScrambleRuntime = nil
        genv.OutlawSetAutoScramble = nil
        genv.OutlawSetAutoBuyScrambleShop = nil
    end
    if v2242Fuse then pcall(v2242Fuse.destroy, v2242Fuse) end
    if genv.OutlawFuseRuntime == v2242Fuse then genv.OutlawFuseRuntime = nil end
    genv.OutlawFuseStatus = nil
    if v2243Mutate then pcall(v2243Mutate.destroy, v2243Mutate) end
    if genv.OutlawAutoMutateRuntime == v2243Mutate then genv.OutlawAutoMutateRuntime = nil end
    genv.OutlawAutoMutateStatus = nil
end
genv.OutlawRuntimeCleanup = v632RuntimeCleanup

local function areaOptions()
    local t = { "None" }
    local ok = pcall(function()
        for k, v in pairs(Areas.Directory) do
            if type(v) == "table" and type(v.DropTable) == "table" then
                t[#t + 1] = (v.DisplayName or k)
            end
        end
    end)
    if #t <= 1 then
        for _, a in ipairs({ "Forest","Lake","Jungle","Desert","Snow","Volcano","Abyss Ocean","Prehistoric","Cosmic","Cherry Blossom","Titan Temple","Angels & Demons" }) do t[#t + 1] = a end
    end
    table.sort(t, function(a, b)
        if a == "None" then return true end
        if b == "None" then return false end
        return a < b
    end)
    return t
end
local function rarityOptions()
    local t = { "None" }
    for _, r in ipairs(v631Rarities) do t[#t + 1] = r end
    return t
end
local areaOpts = areaOptions()
local rarityOpts = rarityOptions()

StealTab:CreateDropdown({
    Name = "Select Area", Options = areaOpts, MultipleOptions = true,
    CurrentOption = zeroinConfig.StealAreas or { "None" }, Flag = "StealAreas",
    Callback = function(v)
        zeroinConfig.StealAreas = v
        if v1327Runtime then
            local t = {}
            for _, a in ipairs(v) do if a ~= "None" then t[#t + 1] = (a == "Angels & Demons" and "Light Dark" or a) end end
            v1327Runtime:setFilter("areas", t)
            v1327Runtime:setFilterEnabled("area", #t > 0)
        end
    end,
})
StealTab:CreateDropdown({
    Name = "Filter By Rarity", Options = rarityOpts, MultipleOptions = true,
    CurrentOption = zeroinConfig.StealRarities or { "None" }, Flag = "StealRarities",
    Callback = function(v)
        zeroinConfig.StealRarities = v
        if v1327Runtime then
            local t = {}
            for _, r in ipairs(v) do if r ~= "None" then t[#t + 1] = r end end
            v1327Runtime:setFilter("rarities", t)
            v1327Runtime:setFilterEnabled("rarity", #t > 0)
        end
    end,
})

for _, n in ipairs(v631.getNames()) do nameOpts[#nameOpts + 1] = n end

local WebhookTab = Window:CreateTab({ Name = "Webhook", Icon = "webhook", Desc = "Discord logs" })
WebhookTab:CreateSection("Discord Webhook")
WebhookTab:CreateToggle({
    Name = "Webhook Logs", CurrentValue = zeroinConfig.WebhookEnabled == true, Flag = "WebhookEnabled",
    Callback = function(v) zeroinConfig.WebhookEnabled = v == true end,
})
WebhookTab:CreateInput({
    Name = "Webhook URL", CurrentValue = zeroinConfig.WebhookURL or "", Flag = "WebhookURL",
    Callback = function(v) zeroinConfig.WebhookURL = tostring(v or "") end,
})
WebhookTab:CreateButton({
    Name = "Test Webhook", Icon = "send-horizontal",
    Callback = function()
        local url = zeroinConfig.WebhookURL
        if not url or url == "" then
            Airflow:Notify({ Title = "Webhook", Content = "Set a valid URL first.", Icon = "alert-triangle", Type = "Warning", Duration = 3 })
            return
        end
        task.spawn(function()
            local request_ = syn and syn.request or request or http_request or http and http.request or fluxus and fluxus.request
            if type(request_) ~= "function" then
                Airflow:Notify({ Title = "Webhook", Content = "Executor HTTP unavailable.", Icon = "alert-triangle", Type = "Warning", Duration = 3 })
                return
            end
            local payload = HttpService:JSONEncode({
                username = "Outlaw - Steal An Egg",
                embeds = { {
                    title = "Webhook Test Successful",
                    description = "Your Outlaw webhook is configured correctly.",
                    color = 52410,
                    footer = { text = "Outlaw - Steal An Egg" },
                    timestamp = DateTime.now():ToIsoDate()
                } }
            })
            local ok, res = pcall(request_, {
                Url = url, url = url,
                Method = "POST", method = "POST",
                Headers = { ["Content-Type"] = "application/json" },
                headers = { ["Content-Type"] = "application/json" },
                Body = payload, body = payload
            })
            if ok then
                Airflow:Notify({ Title = "Webhook", Content = "Test sent successfully.", Icon = "check", Type = "Success", Duration = 3 })
            else
                Airflow:Notify({ Title = "Webhook", Content = "Send failed.", Icon = "alert-triangle", Type = "Warning", Duration = 3 })
            end
        end)
    end,
})
